import { Bell } from 'lucide-react'

export const DEFAULT_PROFILE_PHOTO_URL = `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}coding-cat.png`

interface AppHeaderProps {
  subtitle?: string
  profileName?: string
  profilePhotoUrl?: string | null
  reminderCount?: number
  onProfileClick?: () => void
  onNotificationClick?: () => void
}

export default function AppHeader({
  subtitle,
  profileName,
  profilePhotoUrl,
  reminderCount = 0,
  onProfileClick,
  onNotificationClick,
}: AppHeaderProps) {
  const displayName = profileName?.trim() || 'Profil'
  const avatarSrc = profilePhotoUrl || DEFAULT_PROFILE_PHOTO_URL

  const buttonStyle =
    'border border-[#252525] bg-[#D9CCFF] shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525] transition-all duration-150 hover:brightness-[1.03] active:translate-x-[2px] active:translate-y-[2px] active:shadow-none'

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 -mx-5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#252525]/15 bg-[#fffaf1]/95 px-5 py-4 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col justify-center">
        <span className="block truncate text-lg font-black leading-tight tracking-tight text-[#252525]">
          Bismillah Kelar
        </span>

        {subtitle && (
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium leading-tight text-[#827b70]">
            {subtitle}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="ml-2.5 flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Notifikasi dan pengingat hari ini"
          onClick={onNotificationClick}
          className={`relative flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-[#252525] ${buttonStyle}`}
        >
          <Bell size={15} strokeWidth={2.4} />

          {reminderCount > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute -right-1 -top-1 flex h-4 min-w-[16px] items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] px-1 text-[9px] font-extrabold leading-none text-[#252525]">
              {reminderCount > 9 ? '9+' : reminderCount}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label={`Profil ${displayName}`}
          onClick={onProfileClick}
          className={`flex h-8 max-w-[108px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg py-0.5 pl-0.5 pr-2.5 text-[#252525] ${buttonStyle}`}
        >
          <span className="flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1]">
            <img
              src={avatarSrc}
              alt="Foto profil"
              className="h-full w-full object-cover"
            />
          </span>

          <span className="truncate text-[11px] font-bold">
            {displayName}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </header>
  )
}
=== C:\Web portofolio\bismillah-kelar-personal project\bismillah-kelar\src\components\layout\BottomNav.tsx ===

import {
  Sun,
  CheckCircle2,
  CalendarDays,
  ChartNoAxesCombined,
  MoreHorizontal,
} from 'lucide-react'

export type PageType =
  | 'today'
  | 'tasks'
  | 'calendar'
  | 'progress'
  | 'more'

interface BottomNavProps {
  activePage: PageType
  onNavigate: (page: PageType) => void
}

const navigationItems = [
  { id: 'today', label: 'Hari Ini', icon: Sun },
  { id: 'tasks', label: 'Target', icon: CheckCircle2 },
  { id: 'calendar', label: 'Kalender', icon: CalendarDays },
  { id: 'progress', label: 'Progres', icon: ChartNoAxesCombined },
  { id: 'more', label: 'Lainnya', icon: MoreHorizontal },
] as const

export default function BottomNav({
  activePage,
  onNavigate,
}: BottomNavProps) {
  return (
    <nav className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 z-50 w-full border-t border-[#e9e1d5] bg-[#fffaf1]">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[480px] items-center justify-around px-2 py-2">
        {navigationItems.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon
          const active = activePage === item.id

          return (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => onNavigate(item.id)}
              className={`flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-xl px-1 py-2 transition-all ${
                active
                  ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                  : 'text-[#77716a] hover:bg-[#f4eee4]'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon size={19} strokeWidth={active ? 2.5 : 1.8} />
              <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold">
                {item.label}
              </span>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  )
}

=== C:\Web portofolio\bismillah-kelar-personal project\bismillah-kelar\src\components\tasks\TaskFormModal.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import {
  Calendar as CalendarIcon,
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Clock,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { useAppData } from '../../hooks/AppContext'
import type { Task } from '../../types/app'

interface TaskFormModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  onSaved?: () => void
}

const categories = [
  'Kuliah',
  'Pribadi',
  'Rutinitas',
  'Kesehatan',
  'Kajian',
]

const priorityOptions: {
  value: Task['priority']
  label: string
  badgeClass: string
}[] = [
  {
    value: 'Tinggi',
    label: 'Tinggi',
    badgeClass: 'bg-[#ffd6d6] text-[#8a1f1f]',
  },
  {
    value: 'Sedang',
    label: 'Sedang',
    badgeClass: 'bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525]',
  },
  {
    value: 'Rendah',
    label: 'Rendah',
    badgeClass: 'bg-[#dcebd5] text-[#2f5228]',
  },
]

const monthNames = [
  'Januari',
  'Februari',
  'Maret',
  'April',
  'Mei',
  'Juni',
  'Juli',
  'Agustus',
  'September',
  'Oktober',
  'November',
  'Desember',
]

const dayHeaders = ['Sen', 'Sel', 'Rab', 'Kam', 'Jum', 'Sab', 'Min']

const hourOptions = Array.from({ length: 24 }, (_, idx) =>
  String(idx).padStart(2, '0'),
)

const minuteOptions = [
  '00',
  '05',
  '10',
  '15',
  '20',
  '25',
  '30',
  '35',
  '40',
  '45',
  '50',
  '55',
]

const quickTimePresets = ['07:00', '10:00', '13:00', '16:00', '20:00']

function getDateString(date: Date) {
  return [
    date.getFullYear(),
    String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
    String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'),
  ].join('-')
}

function parseDateString(dateStr: string) {
  const [y, m, d] = dateStr.split('-').map(Number)
  return new Date(y, (m || 1) - 1, d || 1)
}

function formatReadableDate(dateStr: string) {
  const date = parseDateString(dateStr)
  return `${date.getDate()} ${monthNames[date.getMonth()].slice(0, 3)} ${date.getFullYear()}`
}

type ActivePicker = 'category' | 'priority' | 'date' | 'time' | null

export default function TaskFormModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
}: TaskFormModalProps) {
  const { data, addTask } = useAppData()

  const profileName = data.profile.name.trim() || 'teman'
  const todayString = getDateString(new Date())

  const [newTitle, setNewTitle] = useState('')
  const [newCategory, setNewCategory] = useState('Kuliah')
  const [newPriority, setNewPriority] =
    useState<Task['priority']>('Sedang')
  const [newDate, setNewDate] = useState(todayString)
  const [newTime, setNewTime] = useState('20:00')

  const [activePicker, setActivePicker] = useState<ActivePicker>(null)
  const [pickerMonth, setPickerMonth] = useState(() => {
    const now = new Date()
    return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1)
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return

    const now = new Date()
    setNewTitle('')
    setNewCategory('Kuliah')
    setNewPriority('Sedang')
    setNewDate(getDateString(now))
    setNewTime('20:00')
    setActivePicker(null)
    setPickerMonth(new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1))
  }, [isOpen])

  if (!isOpen) return null

  const togglePicker = (picker: ActivePicker) => {
    if (picker === 'date') {
      const selected = parseDateString(newDate)
      setPickerMonth(
        new Date(selected.getFullYear(), selected.getMonth(), 1),
      )
    }
    setActivePicker((prev) => (prev === picker ? null : picker))
  }

  function saveTask() {
    if (!newTitle.trim()) return

    const newTask: Task = {
      id: Date.now(),
      title: newTitle.trim(),
      category: newCategory,
      priority: newPriority,
      date: newDate,
      time: newTime,
      status: 'todo',
    }

    addTask(newTask)
    onClose()
    onSaved?.()
  }

  // Bangun grid kalender untuk custom date picker
  const year = pickerMonth.getFullYear()
  const month = pickerMonth.getMonth()
  const firstDayOfMonth = new Date(year, month, 1).getDay()
  const startOffset = (firstDayOfMonth + 6) % 7 // Senin = 0
  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month + 1, 0).getDate()

  const calendarCells: (number | null)[] = [
    ...Array(startOffset).fill(null),
    ...Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }, (_, i) => i + 1),
  ]

  const [selectedHour, selectedMinute] = newTime.split(':')
  const currentPriorityMeta =
    priorityOptions.find((p) => p.value === newPriority) ??
    priorityOptions[1]

  return createPortal(
    <div
      onClick={onClose}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-end justify-center bg-[#252525]/40 backdrop-blur-sm sm:items-center sm:px-4"
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-[480px] space-y-4 overflow-y-auto rounded-t-2xl border-t-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 pb-8 shadow-2xl sm:rounded-2xl sm:border-2 sm:shadow-[5px_5px_0_#252525]"
      >
        <div className="mx-auto h-1 w-12 rounded-full bg-[#d8d0c4] sm:hidden" />

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">
            Target Baru {profileName}
          </h2>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Tutup modal"
            className="rounded-full p-2 hover:bg-[#f0ece4]"
          >
            <X size={19} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Judul Target */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
            Judul Target
          </label>

          <textarea
            autoFocus
            rows={1}
            enterKeyHint="done"
            value={newTitle}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setNewTitle(event.target.value.replace(/\r?\n/g, ''))
            }
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                event.preventDefault()
                event.currentTarget.blur()
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Misal: Review Bab 6..."
            className="w-full resize-none overflow-x-auto whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-3.5 py-3 text-sm text-[#252525] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#9c9489] focus:bg-[#fffdf7] focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Baris 1: Kategori & Prioritas */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {/* Custom Dropdown Kategori */}
          <div className="relative space-y-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
              Kategori
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => togglePicker('category')}
              aria-expanded={activePicker === 'category'}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] px-3 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
            >
              <span>{newCategory}</span>
              <ChevronDown
                size={15}
                className={`transition-transform ${
                  activePicker === 'category' ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                }`}
              />
            </button>

            {activePicker === 'category' && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-[80] mt-2 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-1.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
                {categories.map((category) => (
                  <button
                    key={category}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setNewCategory(category)
                      setActivePicker(null)
                    }}
                    className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-xs transition ${
                      newCategory === category
                        ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] font-extrabold text-[#252525]'
                        : 'font-medium text-[#625d55] hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {category}
                    {newCategory === category && <Check size={14} />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Custom Dropdown Prioritas */}
          <div className="relative space-y-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
              Prioritas
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => togglePicker('priority')}
              aria-expanded={activePicker === 'priority'}
              className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] px-3 py-3 text-xs font-bold shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none ${currentPriorityMeta.badgeClass}`}
            >
              <span>{currentPriorityMeta.label}</span>
              <ChevronDown
                size={15}
                className={`transition-transform ${
                  activePicker === 'priority' ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                }`}
              />
            </button>

            {activePicker === 'priority' && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-full z-[80] mt-2 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-1.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
                {priorityOptions.map((option) => (
                  <button
                    key={option.value}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setNewPriority(option.value)
                      setActivePicker(null)
                    }}
                    className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-xs transition ${
                      newPriority === option.value
                        ? `${option.badgeClass} font-extrabold`
                        : 'font-medium text-[#625d55] hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span>{option.label}</span>
                    {newPriority === option.value && <Check size={14} />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Baris 2: Custom Tanggal & Custom Waktu */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {/* Custom Date Trigger */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
              Tanggal
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => togglePicker('date')}
              aria-expanded={activePicker === 'date'}
              className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] px-3 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none ${
                activePicker === 'date' ? 'bg-[#ffe69a]' : 'bg-[#f5f0e7]'
              }`}
            >
              <span className="truncate">{formatReadableDate(newDate)}</span>
              <CalendarIcon size={15} className="shrink-0" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Custom Time Trigger */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-semibold">
              Waktu
            </label>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => togglePicker('time')}
              aria-expanded={activePicker === 'time'}
              className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] px-3 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none ${
                activePicker === 'time' ? 'bg-[#ffe69a]' : 'bg-[#f5f0e7]'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{newTime} WIB</span>
              <Clock size={15} className="shrink-0" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Panel Custom Date Picker */}
        {activePicker === 'date' && (
          <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-3.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setPickerMonth(new Date(year, month - 1, 1))
                }
                aria-label="Bulan sebelumnya"
                className="rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-[#f5f0e7] p-1.5 hover:bg-[#ffe69a]"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={15} />
              </button>

              <span className="text-xs font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
                {monthNames[month]} {year}
              </span>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setPickerMonth(new Date(year, month + 1, 1))
                }
                aria-label="Bulan berikutnya"
                className="rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-[#f5f0e7] p-1.5 hover:bg-[#ffe69a]"
              >
                <ChevronRight size={15} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-1.5 grid grid-cols-7 text-center text-[10px] font-bold text-[#716b60]">
              {dayHeaders.map((day) => (
                <div key={day}>{day}</div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 text-center">
              {calendarCells.map((day, idx) => {
                if (!day) {
                  return <div key={`empty-${idx}`} />
                }

                const cellDateStr = `${year}-${String(month + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`
                const isSelected = cellDateStr === newDate
                const isToday = cellDateStr === getDateString(new Date())

                return (
                  <button
                    key={cellDateStr}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setNewDate(cellDateStr)
                      setActivePicker(null)
                    }}
                    className={`rounded-lg py-1.5 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525] shadow-[1.5px_1.5px_0_#252525]'
                        : isToday
                          ? 'bg-[#eee8d9] text-[#252525]'
                          : 'text-[#4e4941] hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {day}
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#eee8d9] pt-2.5">
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const today = new Date()
                    setNewDate(getDateString(today))
                    setActivePicker(null)
                  }}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold"
                >
                  Hari Ini
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    const tomorrow = new Date()
                    tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1)
                    setNewDate(getDateString(tomorrow))
                    setActivePicker(null)
                  }}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-[#d9cfbd] bg-[#f5f0e7] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold"
                >
                  Besok
                </button>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActivePicker(null)}
                className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#716b60] hover:text-[#252525]"
              >
                Tutup
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Panel Custom Time Picker */}
        {activePicker === 'time' && (
          <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-3.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
              {quickTimePresets.map((preset) => (
                <button
                  key={preset}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setNewTime(preset)
                    setActivePicker(null)
                  }}
                  className={`rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition ${
                    newTime === preset
                      ? 'border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525]'
                      : 'border-[#d9cfbd] bg-[#f5f0e7] text-[#5e584f] hover:bg-[#eee8d9]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {preset}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
              <div>
                <p className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#716b60]">
                  Jam
                </p>
                <div className="grid max-h-32 grid-cols-4 gap-1 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-[#e5ddcf] bg-[#fffaf1] p-1.5">
                  {hourOptions.map((hr) => (
                    <button
                      key={hr}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() =>
                        setNewTime(`${hr}:${selectedMinute || '00'}`)
                      }
                      className={`rounded py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                        selectedHour === hr
                          ? 'bg-[#252525] text-white'
                          : 'text-[#4e4941] hover:bg-[#eee8d9]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {hr}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#716b60]">
                  Menit
                </p>
                <div className="grid max-h-32 grid-cols-3 gap-1 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-[#e5ddcf] bg-[#fffaf1] p-1.5">
                  {minuteOptions.map((min) => (
                    <button
                      key={min}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setNewTime(`${selectedHour || '20'}:${min}`)
                        setActivePicker(null)
                      }}
                      className={`rounded py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                        selectedMinute === min
                          ? 'bg-[#252525] text-white'
                          : 'text-[#4e4941] hover:bg-[#eee8d9]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {min}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActivePicker(null)}
                className="rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold"
              >
                Selesai ({newTime})
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tombol Aksi */}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-[#77716a] hover:bg-[#f0ece4]"
          >
            Batal
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={saveTask}
            disabled={!newTitle.trim()}
            className="rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#252525] px-5 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-[3px_3px_0_#fae195] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            Simpan Target
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body,
  )
}

const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, '0')

export function getIndonesiaEvents(date: Date): NationalEvent[] {
  const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}`

  const official = officialEvents2026[key] ?? []

  const observances = nationalObservances
    .filter(
      (event) =>
        event.month === date.getMonth() + 1 &&
        event.day === date.getDate(),
    )
    .map((event) => ({
      name: event.name,
      type: 'peringatan' as const,
    }))

  // Hindari menampilkan nama peringatan yang sama dua kali.
  const combined = [...official, ...observances]

  return combined.filter(
    (event, index) =>
      combined.findIndex((item) => item.name === event.name) === index,
  )
}

import {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from 'react'

import {
  type AppData,
  type UserProfile,
  type Task,
  type AgendaItem,
  type Habit,
  type AppSettings,
  type DailyRecord,
  type FocusTimerState,
} from '../types/app'

import {
  getAppData,
  saveAppData,
  normalizeAppData,
} from '../lib/storage'

import {
  primeTimerEndSound,
  playTimerEndSound,
  stopTimerEndSound,
  startBackgroundTimerKeepAlive,
  stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive,
  saveProfilePhoto,
  getProfilePhoto,
  deleteProfilePhoto,
} from '../lib/audioStorage'

const FOCUS_TIMER_STORAGE_KEY = 'bismillah-kelar-focus-timer'
const SENT_REMINDERS_STORAGE_KEY = 'bismillah-kelar-sent-reminders'
const TIMER_NOTIFICATION_TAG = 'bismillah-kelar-timer'

const MAX_PROFILE_PHOTO_SIZE = 2 * 1024 * 1024

const ALLOWED_PROFILE_PHOTO_TYPES = [
  'image/jpeg',
  'image/png',
  'image/webp',
]

function getMinutes(value: string, fallback: number) {
  const parsed = Number.parseInt(value, 10)
  return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : fallback
}

function getInitialFocusTimer(focusDuration: string): FocusTimerState {
  const defaultMinutes = getMinutes(focusDuration, 25)

  const defaultTimer: FocusTimerState = {
    mode: 'focus',
    status: 'idle',
    endsAt: null,
    remainingSeconds: defaultMinutes * 60,
    sessionMinutes: defaultMinutes,
  }

  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(FOCUS_TIMER_STORAGE_KEY)

    if (!saved) return defaultTimer

    const parsed = JSON.parse(saved) as Partial<FocusTimerState>

    if (
      (parsed.mode !== 'focus' && parsed.mode !== 'break') ||
      !['idle', 'running', 'paused'].includes(parsed.status ?? '') ||
      typeof parsed.remainingSeconds !== 'number'
    ) {
      return defaultTimer
    }

    return {
      mode: parsed.mode,
      status: parsed.status as FocusTimerState['status'],
      endsAt:
        typeof parsed.endsAt === 'number' ? parsed.endsAt : null,
      remainingSeconds: Math.max(0, parsed.remainingSeconds),
      sessionMinutes:
        typeof parsed.sessionMinutes === 'number'
          ? parsed.sessionMinutes
          : defaultMinutes,
    }
  } catch {
    return defaultTimer
  }
}

function formatDateKey(timestamp: number = Date.now()) {
  const date = new Date(timestamp)
  const year = date.getFullYear()
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')

  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`
}

function formatTimeHHMM(date: Date) {
  const hours = String(date.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')
  const minutes = String(date.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')
  return `${hours}:${minutes}`
}

function parseTimeToMinutes(timeStr: string): number | null {
  const match = timeStr.match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/)
  if (!match) return null

  const hours = Number(match[1])
  const minutes = Number(match[2])

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(hours) ||
    !Number.isInteger(minutes) ||
    hours < 0 ||
    hours > 23 ||
    minutes < 0 ||
    minutes > 59
  ) {
    return null
  }

  return hours * 60 + minutes
}

function getSentReminders(): Set<string> {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SENT_REMINDERS_STORAGE_KEY)
    if (!raw) return new Set()
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? new Set(parsed.map(String)) : new Set()
  } catch {
    return new Set()
  }
}

function markReminderSent(key: string) {
  try {
    const sent = getSentReminders()
    sent.add(key)
    const trimmed = Array.from(sent).slice(-200)
    localStorage.setItem(
      SENT_REMINDERS_STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(trimmed),
    )
  } catch {
    // Abaikan jika penyimpanan penuh.
  }
}

function getAudioContextClass() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return undefined

  return (
    window.AudioContext ||
    (
      window as typeof window & {
        webkitAudioContext?: typeof AudioContext
      }
    ).webkitAudioContext
  )
}

// Mengelola suara cadangan jika file audio utama gagal dimuat.
let sharedAudioContext: AudioContext | null = null
let fallbackAudioTimeout: number | null = null
let fallbackAudioInterval: number | null = null
let fallbackPlaybackCallback: ((playing: boolean) => void) | null = null

function unlockFallbackAudioContext() {
  try {
    const AudioContextClass = getAudioContextClass()
    if (!AudioContextClass) return

    if (!sharedAudioContext || sharedAudioContext.state === 'closed') {
      sharedAudioContext = new AudioContextClass()
    }

    if (sharedAudioContext.state === 'suspended') {
      void sharedAudioContext.resume().catch(() => {})
    }
  } catch {
    // Abaikan jika perangkat membatasi AudioContext.
  }
}

function stopTimerSound() {
  if (fallbackAudioInterval !== null) {
    window.clearInterval(fallbackAudioInterval)
    fallbackAudioInterval = null
  }

  if (fallbackAudioTimeout !== null) {
    window.clearTimeout(fallbackAudioTimeout)
    fallbackAudioTimeout = null
  }

  const callback = fallbackPlaybackCallback
  fallbackPlaybackCallback = null
  callback?.(false)
}

// Suara cadangan jika MP3 belum tersedia atau gagal diputar.
function playTimerSound(
  volume: number = 1,
  onPlaybackChange?: (playing: boolean) => void,
  loop: boolean = false,
) {
  try {
    stopTimerSound()

    const safeVolume = Number.isFinite(volume)
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume))
      : 1

    if (safeVolume <= 0) {
      onPlaybackChange?.(false)
      return
    }

    const AudioContextClass = getAudioContextClass()
    if (!AudioContextClass) return

    if (!sharedAudioContext || sharedAudioContext.state === 'closed') {
      sharedAudioContext = new AudioContextClass()
    }

    const audioContext = sharedAudioContext

    if (audioContext.state === 'suspended') {
      void audioContext.resume().catch(() => {})
    }

    fallbackPlaybackCallback = onPlaybackChange ?? null
    onPlaybackChange?.(true)

    const peakGain = 0.28 * safeVolume

    const playTone = (
      frequency: number,
      startTime: number,
      duration: number,
    ) => {
      const oscillator = audioContext.createOscillator()
      const gain = audioContext.createGain()

      oscillator.type = 'sine'
      oscillator.frequency.value = frequency

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, startTime)
      gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(
        peakGain,
        startTime + 0.03,
      )
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(
        peakGain,
        startTime + duration - 0.05,
      )
      gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(
        0,
        startTime + duration,
      )

      oscillator.connect(gain)
      gain.connect(audioContext.destination)

      oscillator.start(startTime)
      oscillator.stop(startTime + duration)

      oscillator.onended = () => {
        oscillator.disconnect()
        gain.disconnect()
      }
    }

    const triggerChime = () => {
      if (audioContext.state === 'closed') return
      if (audioContext.state === 'suspended') {
        void audioContext.resume().catch(() => {})
      }

      const startTime = audioContext.currentTime
      playTone(660, startTime, 0.2)
      playTone(880, startTime + 0.25, 0.25)
      playTone(1046, startTime + 0.55, 0.4)
    }

    triggerChime()

    if (loop) {
      fallbackAudioInterval = window.setInterval(triggerChime, 1600)
    } else {
      fallbackAudioTimeout = window.setTimeout(() => {
        fallbackAudioTimeout = null
        const callback = fallbackPlaybackCallback
        fallbackPlaybackCallback = null
        callback?.(false)
      }, 1100)
    }
  } catch (error) {
    stopTimerSound()
    console.warn('Suara timer tidak dapat diputar:', error)
  }
}

// Mengirim notifikasi perangkat (mendukung browser desktop maupun PWA Android di latar belakang).
export function sendDeviceNotification(
  title: string,
  body: string,
  tag?: string,
  urgent: boolean = false,
): Notification | null {
  if (
    typeof window === 'undefined' ||
    !('Notification' in window) ||
    Notification.permission !== 'granted'
  ) {
    return null
  }

  const options: NotificationOptions & {
    renotify?: boolean
    vibrate?: number[]
  } = {
    body,
    tag,
    requireInteraction: urgent,
    renotify: urgent,
    vibrate: urgent ? [400, 200, 400, 200, 600] : [200, 100, 200],
  }

  if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
    void navigator.serviceWorker.ready
      .then((registration) => registration.showNotification(title, options))
      .catch(() => {})
  }

  try {
    return new Notification(title, options)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

// Web Worker ringan agar detak pemeriksaan timer tidak diperlambat browser saat pindah tab/aplikasi.
function createUnthrottledTicker(onTick: () => void): () => void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof Worker === 'undefined') {
    const id = window.setInterval(onTick, 250)
    return () => window.clearInterval(id)
  }

  try {
    const workerCode = `
      let timer = null;
      self.onmessage = (e) => {
        if (e.data === 'start') {
          if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
          timer = setInterval(() => self.postMessage('tick'), 250);
        } else if (e.data === 'stop') {
          if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
          timer = null;
        }
      };
    `
    const blob = new Blob([workerCode], { type: 'application/javascript' })
    const workerUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
    const worker = new Worker(workerUrl)

    worker.onmessage = () => {
      onTick()
    }
    worker.postMessage('start')

    return () => {
      worker.postMessage('stop')
      worker.terminate()
      URL.revokeObjectURL(workerUrl)
    }
  } catch {
    const id = window.setInterval(onTick, 250)
    return () => window.clearInterval(id)
  }
}

interface AppContextType {
  data: AppData

  profilePhotoUrl: string | null
  updateProfile: (updates: Pick<Partial<UserProfile>, 'name'>) => void
  updateProfilePhoto: (file: File) => Promise<void>
  removeProfilePhoto: () => Promise<void>

  focusTimer: FocusTimerState
  startFocusTimer: () => void
  pauseFocusTimer: () => void
  resetFocusTimer: () => void

  hasActiveCompletionAlert: boolean
  stopCompletionAlert: () => void

  addTask: (task: Task) => void
  updateTask: (id: number, updates: Partial<Task>) => void
  deleteTask: (id: number) => void
  addHabit: (habit: Omit<Habit, 'id'>) => void
  updateHabit: (id: number, updates: Partial<Habit>) => void
  deleteHabit: (id: number) => void

  addAgenda: (date: string, item: AgendaItem) => void
  updateAgenda: (
    date: string,
    id: number,
    updates: Partial<AgendaItem>,
  ) => void
  deleteAgenda: (date: string, id: number) => void

  updateDailyRecord: (
    date: string,
    updates: Partial<DailyRecord>,
  ) => void

  updateSettings: (settings: Partial<AppSettings>) => void
  replaceData: (newData: AppData) => void
}

const AppContext = createContext<AppContextType | undefined>(undefined)

export function AppProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<AppData>(() => getAppData())

  const [profilePhotoUrl, setProfilePhotoUrl] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const profilePhotoUrlRef = useRef<string | null>(null)

  const [focusTimer, setFocusTimer] = useState<FocusTimerState>(() =>
    getInitialFocusTimer(data.settings.focusDuration),
  )

  const [isCompletionSoundPlaying, setIsCompletionSoundPlaying] =
    useState(false)

  const [isCompletionNotificationActive, setIsCompletionNotificationActive] =
    useState(false)

  const completedTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null)
  const activeNotificationRef = useRef<Notification | null>(null)
  const completionPlaybackIdRef = useRef(0)

  const hasActiveCompletionAlert =
    isCompletionSoundPlaying || isCompletionNotificationActive

  // Simpan data utama aplikasi.
  useEffect(() => {
    saveAppData(data)
  }, [data])

  // Memuat foto profil dari IndexedDB saat aplikasi dibuka.
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false

    async function loadProfilePhoto() {
      try {
        const photo = await getProfilePhoto()

        if (!photo || cancelled) return

        const url = URL.createObjectURL(photo)

        if (cancelled) {
          URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
          return
        }

        profilePhotoUrlRef.current = url
        setProfilePhotoUrl(url)

        setData((current) => ({
          ...current,
          profile: {
            ...current.profile,
            photo: 'indexeddb',
          },
        }))
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Gagal memuat foto profil:', error)
      }
    }

    void loadProfilePhoto()

    return () => {
      cancelled = true

      if (profilePhotoUrlRef.current) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(profilePhotoUrlRef.current)
        profilePhotoUrlRef.current = null
      }
    }
  }, [])

  // Simpan timer secara terpisah agar tidak hilang saat berpindah halaman.
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        FOCUS_TIMER_STORAGE_KEY,
        JSON.stringify(focusTimer),
      )
    } catch {
      // Abaikan jika penyimpanan penuh atau tidak tersedia.
    }
  }, [focusTimer])

  // Kelola background keep-alive agar timer tidak dibekukan HP saat keluar dari aplikasi.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusTimer.status === 'running') {
      startBackgroundTimerKeepAlive(focusTimer.mode)
    } else {
      stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive()
    }
  }, [focusTimer.status, focusTimer.mode])

  // Sinkronkan durasi timer jika pengaturan diubah saat status idle.
  useEffect(() => {
    setFocusTimer((current) => {
      if (current.status !== 'idle') return current

      const targetMinutes = getMinutes(
        current.mode === 'focus'
          ? data.settings.focusDuration
          : data.settings.breakDuration,
        current.mode === 'focus' ? 25 : 5,
      )

      const targetSeconds = targetMinutes * 60

      if (
        current.sessionMinutes === targetMinutes &&
        current.remainingSeconds === targetSeconds
      ) {
        return current
      }

      return {
        ...current,
        remainingSeconds: targetSeconds,
        sessionMinutes: targetMinutes,
      }
    })
  }, [data.settings.focusDuration, data.settings.breakDuration])

  // Mengganti URL foto profil yang sedang ditampilkan.
  function setProfilePhoto(blob: Blob | null) {
    if (profilePhotoUrlRef.current) {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(profilePhotoUrlRef.current)
      profilePhotoUrlRef.current = null
    }

    if (!blob) {
      setProfilePhotoUrl(null)
      return
    }

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)

    profilePhotoUrlRef.current = url
    setProfilePhotoUrl(url)
  }

  // Memperbarui nama profil.
  function updateProfile(
    updates: Pick<Partial<UserProfile>, 'name'>,
  ) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      profile: {
        ...current.profile,
        ...updates,
      },
    }))
  }

  // Menyimpan foto profil ke IndexedDB.
  async function updateProfilePhoto(file: File) {
    if (!ALLOWED_PROFILE_PHOTO_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {
      throw new Error('Format foto harus JPG, PNG, atau WebP.')
    }

    if (file.size > MAX_PROFILE_PHOTO_SIZE) {
      throw new Error('Ukuran foto maksimal 2 MB.')
    }

    await saveProfilePhoto(file)

    setProfilePhoto(file)

    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      profile: {
        ...current.profile,
        photo: 'indexeddb',
      },
    }))
  }

  // Menghapus foto profil dari IndexedDB.
  async function removeProfilePhoto() {
    await deleteProfilePhoto()

    setProfilePhoto(null)

    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      profile: {
        ...current.profile,
        photo: null,
      },
    }))
  }

  // Menghentikan suara yang terus berbunyi dan menutup notifikasi timer.
  function stopCompletionAlert() {
    // Batalkan pemutaran yang mungkin masih menunggu data audio.
    completionPlaybackIdRef.current += 1

    stopTimerEndSound()
    stopTimerSound()
    setIsCompletionSoundPlaying(false)

    if (activeNotificationRef.current) {
      activeNotificationRef.current.close()
      activeNotificationRef.current = null
    }

    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      void navigator.serviceWorker.ready
        .then((registration) =>
          registration.getNotifications({ tag: TIMER_NOTIFICATION_TAG }),
        )
        .then((notifications) => {
          notifications.forEach((item) => item.close())
        })
        .catch(() => {})
    }

    setIsCompletionNotificationActive(false)
  }

  // Menampilkan notifikasi timer yang bisa ditutup dari aplikasi.
  function showCompletionNotification(title: string, body: string) {
    if (
      typeof window === 'undefined' ||
      !('Notification' in window) ||
      Notification.permission !== 'granted'
    ) {
      return
    }

    const notification = sendDeviceNotification(
      title,
      body,
      TIMER_NOTIFICATION_TAG,
      true,
    )

    setIsCompletionNotificationActive(true)

    if (!notification) {
      return
    }

    activeNotificationRef.current?.close()
    activeNotificationRef.current = notification

    notification.onclose = () => {
      if (activeNotificationRef.current === notification) {
        activeNotificationRef.current = null
        setIsCompletionNotificationActive(false)
      }
    }

    notification.onerror = () => {
      if (activeNotificationRef.current === notification) {
        activeNotificationRef.current = null
        setIsCompletionNotificationActive(false)
      }
    }
  }

  // Memutar audio default ("The Phone Lingering") / MP3 pilihan secara berulang hingga dihentikan.
  async function playCompletionSound(volume: number) {
    const playbackId = ++completionPlaybackIdRef.current

    stopTimerSound()

    try {
      const played = await playTimerEndSound(
        volume,
        setIsCompletionSoundPlaying,
        true,
      )

      // Jangan putar suara cadangan jika proses sudah dibatalkan.
      if (playbackId !== completionPlaybackIdRef.current) {
        return
      }

      if (!played) {
        playTimerSound(volume, setIsCompletionSoundPlaying, true)
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (playbackId !== completionPlaybackIdRef.current) {
        return
      }

      console.warn('Gagal memutar audio timer:', error)
      playTimerSound(volume, setIsCompletionSoundPlaying, true)
    }
  }

  // Pengingat otomatis jadwal, tenggat tugas, dan rutinitas harian via notifikasi perangkat.
  useEffect(() => {
    const checkScheduledReminders = () => {
      if (
        typeof window === 'undefined' ||
        !('Notification' in window) ||
        Notification.permission !== 'granted'
      ) {
        return
      }

      const now = new Date()
      const todayKey = formatDateKey(now.getTime())
      const currentHHMM = formatTimeHHMM(now)
      const currentTotalMinutes = now.getHours() * 60 + now.getMinutes()
      const sent = getSentReminders()

      // 1. Pengingat tugas harian pukul 07:00
      if (
        data.settings.notifications.daily &&
        currentHHMM === '07:00'
      ) {
        const key = `daily-${todayKey}`
        if (!sent.has(key)) {
          const todayTasksCount = data.tasks.filter(
            (t) => t.date === todayKey && t.status === 'todo',
          ).length
          sendDeviceNotification(
            'Bismillah, mulai harimu!',
            todayTasksCount > 0
              ? `Kamu punya ${todayTasksCount} target tugas untuk diselesaikan hari ini.`
              : 'Yuk cek agenda dan target produktivitasmu hari ini.',
            key,
          )
          markReminderSent(key)
        }
      }

      // 2. Pengingat agenda hari ini saat jam jadwal tiba
      if (data.settings.notifications.daily) {
        const todayAgendas = data.agenda[todayKey] ?? []
        for (const item of todayAgendas) {
          if (item.done) continue
          const itemMinutes = parseTimeToMinutes(item.time)
          if (itemMinutes !== null && itemMinutes === currentTotalMinutes) {
            const key = `agenda-${todayKey}-${item.id}-${item.time}`
            if (!sent.has(key)) {
              sendDeviceNotification(
                `Jadwal Dimulai: ${item.title}`,
                `Pukul ${item.time} WIB â€¢ ${item.category}`,
                key,
              )
              markReminderSent(key)
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // 3. Pengingat deadline tugas (2 jam sebelum tenggat & saat waktu tenggat tiba)
      if (data.settings.notifications.deadline) {
        const todayTasks = data.tasks.filter(
          (t) => t.date === todayKey && t.status === 'todo',
        )
        for (const task of todayTasks) {
          const taskMinutes = parseTimeToMinutes(task.time)
          if (taskMinutes === null) continue

          const diffMinutes = taskMinutes - currentTotalMinutes

          if (diffMinutes === 120) {
            const key = `deadline-2h-${todayKey}-${task.id}-${task.time}`
            if (!sent.has(key)) {
              sendDeviceNotification(
                `2 Jam Menuju Tenggat: ${task.title}`,
                `Batas waktu pukul ${task.time} WIB (${task.priority}).`,
                key,
              )
              markReminderSent(key)
            }
          } else if (diffMinutes === 0) {
            const key = `deadline-now-${todayKey}-${task.id}-${task.time}`
            if (!sent.has(key)) {
              sendDeviceNotification(
                `Batas Waktu Tugas: ${task.title}`,
                `Sudah pukul ${task.time} WIB. Yuk segera tuntaskan!`,
                key,
              )
              markReminderSent(key)
            }
          }
        }
      }

      // 4. Pengingat refleksi malam hari pukul 21:30
      if (
        data.settings.notifications.reflection &&
        currentHHMM === '21:30'
      ) {
        const key = `reflection-${todayKey}`
        if (!sent.has(key)) {
          sendDeviceNotification(
            'Waktunya Refleksi Malam',
            'Luangkan sejenak untuk mencatat progres dan evaluasi harimu.',
            key,
          )
          markReminderSent(key)
        }
      }
    }

    checkScheduledReminders()

    const interval = window.setInterval(checkScheduledReminders, 15000)
    const onVisible = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
        checkScheduledReminders()
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)

    return () => {
      window.clearInterval(interval)
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
    }
  }, [
    data.agenda,
    data.tasks,
    data.settings.notifications.daily,
    data.settings.notifications.deadline,
    data.settings.notifications.reflection,
  ])

  // Timer berjalan di tingkat AppProvider dengan Web Worker agar tetap akurat di luar aplikasi.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusTimer.status !== 'running' || focusTimer.endsAt === null) {
      return
    }

    const timerEndsAt = focusTimer.endsAt
    const timerMode = focusTimer.mode
    const sessionMinutes = focusTimer.sessionMinutes

    const checkTimerCompletion = () => {
      if (Date.now() < timerEndsAt) return

      if (completedTimerRef.current === timerEndsAt) return
      completedTimerRef.current = timerEndsAt

      stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive()
      void playCompletionSound(data.settings.soundVolume ?? 1)

      if (timerMode === 'focus') {
        const completedDateKey = formatDateKey(timerEndsAt)

        setData((current) => {
          const previous = current.dailyRecords[completedDateKey] ?? {
            habitStatus: {},
            note: '',
            focusSessions: 0,
            focusMinutes: 0,
          }

          return {
            ...current,
            dailyRecords: {
              ...current.dailyRecords,
              [completedDateKey]: {
                ...previous,
                focusSessions: previous.focusSessions + 1,
                focusMinutes: previous.focusMinutes + sessionMinutes,
              },
            },
          }
        })

        const breakMinutes = getMinutes(
          data.settings.breakDuration,
          5,
        )

        setFocusTimer({
          mode: 'break',
          status: 'idle',
          endsAt: null,
          remainingSeconds: breakMinutes * 60,
          sessionMinutes: breakMinutes,
        })

        if (data.settings.notifications.focus) {
          showCompletionNotification(
            'Waktunya beristirahat!',
            'Sesi fokus selesai. Saatnya mengambil jeda sejenak.',
          )
        }
     } else {
        const completedDateKey = formatDateKey(timerEndsAt)

        setData((current) => {
          const previous = current.dailyRecords[completedDateKey] ?? {
            habitStatus: {},
            note: '',
            focusSessions: 0,
            focusMinutes: 0,
            breakSessions: 0,
          }

          return {
            ...current,
            dailyRecords: {
              ...current.dailyRecords,
              [completedDateKey]: {
                ...previous,
                breakSessions: (previous.breakSessions ?? 0) + 1,
              },
            },
          }
        })

        const focusMinutes = getMinutes(
          data.settings.focusDuration,
          25,
        )

        setFocusTimer({
          mode: 'focus',
          status: 'idle',
          endsAt: null,
          remainingSeconds: focusMinutes * 60,
          sessionMinutes: focusMinutes,
        })

        if (data.settings.notifications.focus) {
          showCompletionNotification(
            'Waktu istirahat selesai!',
            'Yuk, kembali fokus ke aktivitasmu.',
          )
        }
      }
    }

    checkTimerCompletion()

    const stopTicker = createUnthrottledTicker(checkTimerCompletion)
    const handleVisibilityChange = () => {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
        checkTimerCompletion()
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange)
    window.addEventListener('focus', checkTimerCompletion)
    window.addEventListener('pageshow', checkTimerCompletion)

    return () => {
      stopTicker()
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange)
      window.removeEventListener('focus', checkTimerCompletion)
      window.removeEventListener('pageshow', checkTimerCompletion)
    }
  }, [
    focusTimer.status,
    focusTimer.endsAt,
    focusTimer.mode,
    focusTimer.sessionMinutes,
    data.settings.breakDuration,
    data.settings.focusDuration,
    data.settings.notifications.focus,
    data.settings.soundVolume,
  ])

  function startFocusTimer() {
    // Pastikan audio alarm yang berbunyi otomatis mati sebelum sesi dimulai.
    stopCompletionAlert()

    // Buka izin elemen audio & AudioContext selama interaksi pengguna agar tetap bisa berbunyi di luar aplikasi.
    void primeTimerEndSound()
    unlockFallbackAudioContext()
    startBackgroundTimerKeepAlive(focusTimer.mode)

    const nowMs = Date.now()

    setFocusTimer((current) => {
      const isResuming = current.status === 'paused'

      const duration = isResuming
        ? current.remainingSeconds
        : getMinutes(
            current.mode === 'focus'
              ? data.settings.focusDuration
              : data.settings.breakDuration,
            current.mode === 'focus' ? 25 : 5,
          ) * 60

      const sessionMinutes = isResuming
        ? current.sessionMinutes
        : Math.ceil(duration / 60)

      return {
        ...current,
        status: 'running',
        endsAt: nowMs + duration * 1000,
        remainingSeconds: duration,
        sessionMinutes,
      }
    })
  }

  function pauseFocusTimer() {
    stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive()
    const nowMs = Date.now()

    setFocusTimer((current) => {
      if (current.status !== 'running' || current.endsAt === null) {
        return current
      }

      const remaining = Math.max(
        0,
        Math.ceil((current.endsAt - nowMs) / 1000),
      )

      return {
        ...current,
        status: 'paused',
        endsAt: null,
        remainingSeconds: remaining,
      }
    })
  }

  function resetFocusTimer() {
    stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive()
    stopCompletionAlert()

    setFocusTimer((current) => {
      const minutes = getMinutes(
        current.mode === 'focus'
          ? data.settings.focusDuration
          : data.settings.breakDuration,
        current.mode === 'focus' ? 25 : 5,
      )

      return {
        mode: current.mode,
        status: 'idle',
        endsAt: null,
        remainingSeconds: minutes * 60,
        sessionMinutes: minutes,
      }
    })
  }

  function addTask(task: Task) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      tasks: [...current.tasks, task],
    }))
  }

  function updateTask(id: number, updates: Partial<Task>) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      tasks: current.tasks.map((task) =>
        task.id === id ? { ...task, ...updates } : task,
      ),
    }))
  }

  function deleteTask(id: number) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      tasks: current.tasks.filter((task) => task.id !== id),
    }))
  }

  // Menambahkan rutinitas baru.
  function addHabit(habit: Omit<Habit, 'id'>) {
    setData((current) => {
      const nextId =
        current.habits.length > 0
          ? Math.max(...current.habits.map((item) => item.id)) + 1
          : 1

      return {
        ...current,
        habits: [
          ...current.habits,
          {
            ...habit,
            id: nextId,
          },
        ],
      }
    })
  }

  // Mengubah rutinitas.
  function updateHabit(id: number, updates: Partial<Habit>) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      habits: current.habits.map((habit) =>
        habit.id === id
          ? { ...habit, ...updates, id }
          : habit,
      ),
    }))
  }

  // Menghapus rutinitas.
  function deleteHabit(id: number) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      habits: current.habits.filter((habit) => habit.id !== id),
    }))
  }

  function addAgenda(date: string, item: AgendaItem) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      agenda: {
        ...current.agenda,
        [date]: [...(current.agenda[date] ?? []), item],
      },
    }))
  }

  function updateAgenda(
    date: string,
    id: number,
    updates: Partial<AgendaItem>,
  ) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      agenda: {
        ...current.agenda,
        [date]: (current.agenda[date] ?? []).map((item) =>
          item.id === id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item,
        ),
      },
    }))
  }

  function deleteAgenda(date: string, id: number) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      agenda: {
        ...current.agenda,
        [date]: (current.agenda[date] ?? []).filter(
          (item) => item.id !== id,
        ),
      },
    }))
  }

  function updateDailyRecord(
    date: string,
    updates: Partial<DailyRecord>,
  ) {
    setData((current) => {
      const previous = current.dailyRecords[date] ?? {
        habitStatus: {},
        note: '',
        focusSessions: 0,
        focusMinutes: 0,
      }

      return {
        ...current,
        dailyRecords: {
          ...current.dailyRecords,
          [date]: {
            ...previous,
            ...updates,
            habitStatus: {
              ...previous.habitStatus,
              ...updates.habitStatus,
            },
          },
        },
      }
    })
  }

  function updateSettings(settings: Partial<AppSettings>) {
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      settings: {
        ...current.settings,
        ...settings,
        notifications: {
          ...current.settings.notifications,
          ...settings.notifications,
        },
      },
    }))
  }

  function replaceData(newData: AppData) {
    stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive()
    stopCompletionAlert()

    const normalized = normalizeAppData(newData)
    const restoredFocusMinutes = getMinutes(
      normalized.settings.focusDuration,
      25,
    )

    setFocusTimer({
      mode: 'focus',
      status: 'idle',
      endsAt: null,
      remainingSeconds: restoredFocusMinutes * 60,
      sessionMinutes: restoredFocusMinutes,
    })

    setData({
      ...normalized,
      profile: {
        ...normalized.profile,
        photo: profilePhotoUrlRef.current ? 'indexeddb' : null,
      },
    })
  }

  return (
    <AppContext.Provider
      value={{
        data,

        profilePhotoUrl,
        updateProfile,
        updateProfilePhoto,
        removeProfilePhoto,

        focusTimer,
        startFocusTimer,
        pauseFocusTimer,
        resetFocusTimer,

        hasActiveCompletionAlert,
        stopCompletionAlert,

        addTask,
        updateTask,
        deleteTask,

        addHabit,
        updateHabit,
        deleteHabit,

        addAgenda,
        updateAgenda,
        deleteAgenda,

        updateDailyRecord,
        updateSettings,
        replaceData,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AppContext.Provider>
  )
}

export function useAppData() {
  const context = useContext(AppContext)

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useAppData harus digunakan di dalam AppProvider')
  }

  return context
}

const DB_NAME = 'bismillah-kelar-audio'
const STORE_NAME = 'sounds'
const SOUND_KEY = 'timer-end'
const PROFILE_PHOTO_KEY = 'profile-photo'

export const DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY = 'bismillah-kelar-default-alarm-tone'

export type DefaultToneId = 'tone-1' | 'tone-2' | 'tone-3'
export type SelectedToneId = DefaultToneId | 'custom'

export const DEFAULT_ALARM_TONES: Record<
  DefaultToneId,
  { id: DefaultToneId; name: string; url: string }
> = {
  'tone-1': {
    id: 'tone-1',
    name: 'Your Phone Linging',
    url: `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}audio/the-phone-lingering.mp3`,
  },
  'tone-2': {
    id: 'tone-2',
    name: '#jedagjedug - Zachz Winner',
    url: `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}audio/jedagjedug-zachzwinner.mp3`,
  },
  'tone-3': {

    name: 'MONTAGEM XONADA - TheGoodVibe',
    url: `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}audio/phonk-montagem-xonada.mp3`,
  },

export const DEFAULT_TIMER_SOUND_NAME = DEFAULT_ALARM_TONES['tone-1'].name
export const DEFAULT_TIMER_SOUND_URL = DEFAULT_ALARM_TONES['tone-1'].url

export function isCustomTimerSoundSelected(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return false
  }

  return localStorage.getItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY) === 'custom'
}

export function getSelectedDefaultTimerSound(): {
  id: DefaultToneId
  name: string
  url: string
} {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return DEFAULT_ALARM_TONES['tone-1']
  }

  const saved = localStorage.getItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY)
  if (saved === 'tone-2' || saved === 'tone-3') {
    return DEFAULT_ALARM_TONES[saved]
  }

  return DEFAULT_ALARM_TONES['tone-1']
}

export interface TimerSound {
  name: string
  blob: Blob
}

// Elemen audio tunggal yang di-unlock saat user klik Mulai agar tetap bisa berbunyi di latar belakang.
let alarmAudio: HTMLAudioElement | null = null
let activeAudioUrl: string | null = null
let playbackCallback: ((playing: boolean) => void) | null = null
let isAlarmPlaying = false

// Mencegah audio lama diputar setelah perintah stop atau pemutaran baru.
let playbackGeneration = 0

// Audio sunyi (0 amplitudo) untuk menjaga proses timer tetap aktif saat keluar dari aplikasi di HP.
let keepAliveAudio: HTMLAudioElement | null = null
let keepAliveWavUrl: string | null = null

function getSilentWavUrl(): string {
  if (keepAliveWavUrl) return keepAliveWavUrl

  const sampleRate = 8000
  const numSamples = sampleRate // 1 detik sunyi
  const dataSize = numSamples
  const buffer = new ArrayBuffer(44 + dataSize)
  const view = new DataView(buffer)

  const writeString = (offset: number, text: string) => {
    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += 1) {
      view.setUint8(offset + i, text.charCodeAt(i))
    }
  }

  writeString(0, 'RIFF')
  view.setUint32(4, 36 + dataSize, true)
  writeString(8, 'WAVE')
  writeString(12, 'fmt ')
  view.setUint32(16, 16, true) // PCM
  view.setUint16(20, 1, true) // Linear PCM
  view.setUint16(22, 1, true) // Mono
  view.setUint32(24, sampleRate, true)
  view.setUint32(28, sampleRate, true)
  view.setUint16(32, 1, true)
  view.setUint16(34, 8, true) // 8-bit
  writeString(36, 'data')
  view.setUint32(40, dataSize, true)

  // 0x80 adalah titik tengah (hening total) pada PCM 8-bit unsigned.
  new Uint8Array(buffer, 44).fill(0x80)

  const blob = new Blob([buffer], { type: 'audio/wav' })
  keepAliveWavUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  return keepAliveWavUrl
}

export function startBackgroundTimerKeepAlive(
  mode: 'focus' | 'break',
): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return

  try {
    if (!keepAliveAudio) {
      keepAliveAudio = new Audio(getSilentWavUrl())
      keepAliveAudio.loop = true
      keepAliveAudio.preload = 'auto'
    }

    keepAliveAudio.volume = 0.01

    if (keepAliveAudio.paused) {
      void keepAliveAudio.play().catch(() => {})
    }

    if ('mediaSession' in navigator) {
      navigator.mediaSession.metadata = new MediaMetadata({
        title:
          mode === 'focus'
            ? 'Sesi Fokus Berjalan'
            : 'Waktu Istirahat Berjalan',
        artist: 'Bismillah Kelar',
        album: 'Timer Produktivitas',
      })
      navigator.mediaSession.playbackState = 'playing'
    }
  } catch {
    // Abaikan jika perangkat membatasi MediaSession.
  }
}

export function stopBackgroundTimerKeepAlive(): void {
  try {
    if (keepAliveAudio) {
      keepAliveAudio.pause()
      keepAliveAudio.currentTime = 0
    }

    if ('mediaSession' in navigator) {
      navigator.mediaSession.playbackState = 'none'
    }
  } catch {
    // Abaikan error saat menghentikan keep-alive.
  }
}

function getOrCreateAlarmAudio(): HTMLAudioElement {
  if (!alarmAudio) {
    alarmAudio = new Audio()
    alarmAudio.preload = 'auto'
  }
  return alarmAudio
}

function openAudioDB(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, 1)

    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {
      const db = request.result

      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
        db.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME)
      }
    }

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const db = request.result

      db.onversionchange = () => {
        db.close()
      }

      resolve(db)
    }

    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error)
    request.onblocked = () =>
      reject(new Error('Akses penyimpanan IndexedDB sedang diblokir.'))
  })
}

export async function saveTimerSound(file: File): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openAudioDB()

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite')

    transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).put(
      {
        name: file.name,
        blob: file,
      },
      SOUND_KEY,
    )

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      db.close()
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        localStorage.setItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY, 'custom')
      }
      resolve()
    }

    transaction.onerror = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(transaction.error)
    }

    transaction.onabort = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(
        transaction.error ??
          new Error('Transaksi penyimpanan audio dibatalkan.'),
      )
    }
  })
}

export async function getTimerSound(): Promise<TimerSound | null> {
  const db = await openAudioDB()

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly')
    const request = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).get(SOUND_KEY)

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const result = request.result as Partial<TimerSound> | undefined
      db.close()

      if (
        result &&
        typeof result.name === 'string' &&
        result.blob instanceof Blob
      ) {
        resolve({
          name: result.name,
          blob: result.blob,
        })
        return
      }

      resolve(null)
    }

    request.onerror = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(request.error)
    }

    transaction.onabort = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(
        transaction.error ?? new Error('Pembacaan audio dibatalkan.'),
      )
    }
  })
}

export async function deleteTimerSound(): Promise<void> {
  stopTimerEndSound()

  const db = await openAudioDB()

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite')

    transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).delete(SOUND_KEY)

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      db.close()
      if (
        typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
        localStorage.getItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY) === 'custom'
      ) {
        localStorage.setItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY, 'tone-1')
      }
      resolve()
    }

    transaction.onerror = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(transaction.error)
    }

    transaction.onabort = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(
        transaction.error ?? new Error('Penghapusan audio dibatalkan.'),
      )
    }
  })
}

// Membuka izin autoplay elemen audio sesuai nada pilihan aktif (MP3 kustom atau salah satu dari 3 nada default).
export async function primeTimerEndSound(): Promise<void> {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || isAlarmPlaying) return

  try {
    const audio = getOrCreateAlarmAudio()
    const useCustom = isCustomTimerSoundSelected()

    let customSound: TimerSound | null = null
    if (useCustom) {
      try {
        customSound = await getTimerSound()
      } catch {
        customSound = null
      }
    }

    if (isAlarmPlaying) return

    if (activeAudioUrl) {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(activeAudioUrl)
      activeAudioUrl = null
    }

    if (useCustom && customSound) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(customSound.blob)
      activeAudioUrl = blobUrl
      audio.src = blobUrl
    } else {
      audio.src = getSelectedDefaultTimerSound().url
    }

    audio.loop = false
    audio.muted = true
    await audio.play()
    audio.pause()
    audio.currentTime = 0
    audio.muted = false
  } catch {
    if (alarmAudio) {
      alarmAudio.muted = false
    }
  }
}

export function stopTimerEndSound(): void {
  // Membatalkan semua proses pemutaran yang masih tertunda.
  playbackGeneration += 1
  isAlarmPlaying = false

  if (alarmAudio) {
    alarmAudio.loop = false
    alarmAudio.onended = null
    alarmAudio.onerror = null
    alarmAudio.pause()
    alarmAudio.currentTime = 0
    alarmAudio.muted = false
  }

  if (activeAudioUrl) {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(activeAudioUrl)
    activeAudioUrl = null
  }

  const callback = playbackCallback
  playbackCallback = null
  callback?.(false)
}

export async function playTimerEndSound(
  volume: number = 1,
  onPlaybackChange?: (playing: boolean) => void,
  loop: boolean = false,
): Promise<boolean> {
  stopTimerEndSound()

  const currentGeneration = playbackGeneration

  try {
    const useCustom = isCustomTimerSoundSelected()
    let customSound: TimerSound | null = null

    if (useCustom) {
      try {
        customSound = await getTimerSound()
      } catch {
        customSound = null
      }
    }

    // Jangan lanjutkan jika sudah ada perintah stop atau pemutaran baru.
    if (currentGeneration !== playbackGeneration) {
      return false
    }

    const audio = getOrCreateAlarmAudio()
    const isBlobUrl = Boolean(useCustom && customSound)
    const sourceUrl =
      useCustom && customSound
        ? URL.createObjectURL(customSound.blob)
        : getSelectedDefaultTimerSound().url

    if (activeAudioUrl) {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(activeAudioUrl)
      activeAudioUrl = null
    }

    activeAudioUrl = isBlobUrl ? sourceUrl : null
    playbackCallback = onPlaybackChange ?? null

    const safeVolume = Number.isFinite(volume)
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume))
      : 1

    audio.src = sourceUrl
    audio.loop = loop
    audio.volume = safeVolume
    audio.muted = safeVolume === 0
    audio.currentTime = 0

    let cleaned = false

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (cleaned) return
      cleaned = true
      isAlarmPlaying = false

      if (isBlobUrl && activeAudioUrl === sourceUrl) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(sourceUrl)
        activeAudioUrl = null
      }

      const callback = playbackCallback
      playbackCallback = null
      callback?.(false)
    }

    audio.onended = loop ? null : cleanup
    audio.onerror = cleanup

    isAlarmPlaying = true
    await audio.play()

    // Pemutaran mungkin sudah dibatalkan ketika audio.play() menunggu.
    if (currentGeneration !== playbackGeneration) {
      cleanup()
      return false
    }

    onPlaybackChange?.(true)

    return true
  } catch (error) {
    if (currentGeneration === playbackGeneration) {
      stopTimerEndSound()
    }

    console.warn('Suara timer tidak dapat diputar:', error)
    return false
  }
}

export async function saveProfilePhoto(file: File): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openAudioDB()

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite')

    transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).put(file, PROFILE_PHOTO_KEY)

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      db.close()
      resolve()
    }

    transaction.onerror = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(transaction.error)
    }

    transaction.onabort = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(
        transaction.error ??
          new Error('Penyimpanan foto profil dibatalkan.'),
      )
    }
  })
}

export async function getProfilePhoto(): Promise<Blob | null> {
  const db = await openAudioDB()

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readonly')
    const request = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).get(PROFILE_PHOTO_KEY)

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const result = request.result

      db.close()
      resolve(result instanceof Blob ? result : null)
    }

    request.onerror = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(request.error)
    }

    transaction.onabort = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(
        transaction.error ??
          new Error('Pembacaan foto profil dibatalkan.'),
      )
    }
  })
}

export async function deleteProfilePhoto(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openAudioDB()

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction(STORE_NAME, 'readwrite')

    transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME).delete(PROFILE_PHOTO_KEY)

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      db.close()
      resolve()
    }

    transaction.onerror = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(transaction.error)
    }

    transaction.onabort = () => {
      db.close()
      reject(
        transaction.error ??
          new Error('Penghapusan foto profil dibatalkan.'),
      )
    }
  })
}

import {
  initialAppData,
  type AppData,
  type DailyRecord,
} from '../types/app'

const STORAGE_KEY = 'bismillah-kelar-data'

export function normalizeAppData(
  input?: Partial<AppData> | null,
): AppData {
  const saved = input ?? {}

  const savedSettings = saved.settings
  const savedProfile = saved.profile

  const dailyRecords: Record<string, DailyRecord> = {}

  if (
    saved.dailyRecords &&
    typeof saved.dailyRecords === 'object' &&
    !Array.isArray(saved.dailyRecords)
  ) {
    Object.entries(saved.dailyRecords).forEach(([date, record]) => {
      if (!record || typeof record !== 'object') return

      dailyRecords[date] = {
        habitStatus:
          record.habitStatus &&
          typeof record.habitStatus === 'object'
            ? record.habitStatus
            : {},
        note:
          typeof record.note === 'string'
            ? record.note
            : '',
        focusSessions:
          typeof record.focusSessions === 'number'
            ? record.focusSessions
            : 0,
        focusMinutes:
          typeof record.focusMinutes === 'number'
            ? record.focusMinutes
            : 0,
      }
    })
  }

  return {
    profile: {
      name:
        typeof savedProfile?.name === 'string'
          ? savedProfile.name
          : initialAppData.profile.name,

      photo:
        typeof savedProfile?.photo === 'string'
          ? savedProfile.photo
          : null,
    },

    tasks: Array.isArray(saved.tasks)
      ? saved.tasks
      : initialAppData.tasks,

    agenda:
      saved.agenda &&
      typeof saved.agenda === 'object' &&
      !Array.isArray(saved.agenda)
        ? saved.agenda
        : initialAppData.agenda,

    habits: Array.isArray(saved.habits)
      ? saved.habits
      : initialAppData.habits,

    dailyRecords,

    settings: {
      ...initialAppData.settings,
      ...savedSettings,

      notifications: {
        ...initialAppData.settings.notifications,
        ...savedSettings?.notifications,
      },
    },
  }
}

export function getAppData(): AppData {
  try {
    const savedData = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)

    if (!savedData) {
      return normalizeAppData(initialAppData)
    }

    const parsedData = JSON.parse(savedData) as Partial<AppData>

    return normalizeAppData(parsedData)
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Gagal membaca data aplikasi:', error)

    return normalizeAppData(initialAppData)
  }
}

export function saveAppData(data: AppData): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(data),
    )
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Gagal menyimpan data aplikasi:', error)
  }
}

export function clearAppData(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY)
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'

type EditingAgenda = {
  id: number
  date: string

type DeleteTarget = {
  id: number
  date: string
  title: string
