const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, '0')

const dateKey = (date: Date) =>
  `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}`

const monthNames = [
  'Januari', 'Februari', 'Maret', 'April',
  'Mei', 'Juni', 'Juli', 'Agustus',
  'September', 'Oktober', 'November', 'Desember',
]

const weekdayNames = ['Sen', 'Sel', 'Rab', 'Kam', 'Jum', 'Sab', 'Min']

const categories = [
  { name: 'Kuliah', color: '#ffb5a7' },
  { name: 'Tugas', color: '#d9ccff' },
  { name: 'Ibadah', color: '#bdeccf' },
  { name: 'Pribadi', color: '#ffe69a' },
  { name: 'Organisasi', color: '#a9d6e5' },
  { name: 'Lainnya', color: '#d8d0c4' },
]

function formatLongDate(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    weekday: 'long',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(date)
}

function getEventColor(type: string) {
  if (type === 'libur') return '#ba1a1a'
  if (type === 'cuti') return '#d97706'
  return '#3976b9'
}

function getEventLabel(type: string) {
  if (type === 'libur') return 'Libur nasional'
  if (type === 'cuti') return 'Cuti bersama'
  return 'Hari peringatan'
}

function getEventBackground(type: string) {
  if (type === 'libur') return 'border-red-100 bg-red-50'
  if (type === 'cuti') return 'border-orange-100 bg-orange-50'
  return 'border-blue-100 bg-blue-50'
}

function NationalEventList({ date }: { date: Date }) {
  const events = getIndonesiaEvents(date)

  if (events.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      {events.map((event, index) => (
        <div
          key={`${event.name}-${index}`}
          className={`rounded-xl border p-3 ${getEventBackground(event.type)}`}
        >
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <span
              className="mt-1 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full"
              style={{ backgroundColor: getEventColor(event.type) }}
            />

            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[#252525]">
                {event.name}
              </p>
              <p
                className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold"
                style={{ color: getEventColor(event.type) }}
              >
                {getEventLabel(event.type)}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function Calendar() {
  const {
    data,
    addAgenda,
    updateAgenda,
    deleteAgenda,
  } = useAppData()

  const now = new Date()
  const todayKey = dateKey(now)

  const [displayMonth, setDisplayMonth] = useState(
    new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1),
  )

  const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState(todayKey)
  const [view, setView] = useState<CalendarView>('Bulan')
  const [showMonthPicker, setShowMonthPicker] = useState(false)
  const [pickerYear, setPickerYear] = useState(now.getFullYear())

  const [agendaActionMode, setAgendaActionMode] = useState<
    'normal' | 'edit' | 'delete'
  >('normal')

  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
  const [editingAgenda, setEditingAgenda] = useState<EditingAgenda | null>(null)
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<DeleteTarget | null>(null)

  const [formTitle, setFormTitle] = useState('')
  const [formDate, setFormDate] = useState(selectedDate)
  const [formTime, setFormTime] = useState('20:00')
  const [formCategory, setFormCategory] = useState('Pribadi')
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState('')

  const agenda = data.agenda
  const selected = new Date(`${selectedDate}T00:00:00`)
  const selectedAgenda = agenda[selectedDate] ?? []

  const days = useMemo(() => {
    const firstDay = new Date(
      displayMonth.getFullYear(),
      displayMonth.getMonth(),
      1,
    )

    const startOffset = (firstDay.getDay() + 6) % 7
    const startDate = new Date(firstDay)
    startDate.setDate(firstDay.getDate() - startOffset)

    return Array.from({ length: 42 }, (_, index) => {
      const date = new Date(startDate)
      date.setDate(startDate.getDate() + index)

      return {
        date,
        key: dateKey(date),
        number: date.getDate(),
        currentMonth: date.getMonth() === displayMonth.getMonth(),
        today: dateKey(date) === todayKey,
      }
    })
  }, [displayMonth, todayKey])

  function changeMonth(amount: number) {
    setDisplayMonth((current) =>
      new Date(
        current.getFullYear(),
        current.getMonth() + amount,
        1,
      ),
    )
  }

  function openMonthPicker() {
    setPickerYear(displayMonth.getFullYear())
    setShowMonthPicker(true)
  }

  function jumpToMonth(monthIndex: number) {
    const nextMonthDate = new Date(pickerYear, monthIndex, 1)
    setDisplayMonth(nextMonthDate)

    const targetDay = Math.min(
      selected.getDate(),
      new Date(pickerYear, monthIndex + 1, 0).getDate(),
    )
    setSelectedDate(dateKey(new Date(pickerYear, monthIndex, targetDay)))
    setShowMonthPicker(false)
  }

  function selectDay(key: string) {
    setSelectedDate(key)
    setAgendaActionMode('normal')
  }

  function toggleAgendaItem(id: number) {
    const item = selectedAgenda.find(
      (agendaItem) => agendaItem.id === id,
    )

    if (!item) return

    updateAgenda(selectedDate, id, {
      done: !item.done,
    })
  }

  function openAddForm() {
    setAgendaActionMode('normal')
    setEditingAgenda(null)
    setFormTitle('')
    setFormDate(selectedDate)
    setFormTime('20:00')
    setFormCategory('Pribadi')
    setFormError('')
    setShowForm(true)
  }

  function openEditForm(item: AgendaItem) {
    setAgendaActionMode('normal')
    setEditingAgenda({
      id: item.id,
      date: selectedDate,
    })

    setFormTitle(item.title)
    setFormDate(selectedDate)
    setFormTime(item.time)
    setFormCategory(
      categories.some((category) => category.name === item.category)
        ? item.category
        : 'Lainnya',
    )
    setFormError('')
    setShowForm(true)
  }

  function handleAgendaCardClick(item: AgendaItem) {
    if (agendaActionMode === 'edit') {
      openEditForm(item)
      return
    }

    if (agendaActionMode === 'delete') {
      setAgendaActionMode('normal')
      setDeleteTarget({
        id: item.id,
        date: selectedDate,
        title: item.title,
      })
      return
    }

    toggleAgendaItem(item.id)
  }

  function closeForm() {
    setShowForm(false)
    setEditingAgenda(null)
    setFormError('')
  }

  function saveSchedule() {
    if (!formTitle.trim()) {
      setFormError('Nama kegiatan belum diisi.')
      return
    }

    if (!formDate || !formTime) {
      setFormError('Tanggal dan waktu wajib diisi.')
      return
    }

    const category = categories.find(
      (item) => item.name === formCategory,
    )

    const color = category?.color ?? '#d8d0c4'

    if (editingAgenda) {
      const oldItem = (agenda[editingAgenda.date] ?? []).find(
        (item) => item.id === editingAgenda.id,
      )

      if (!oldItem) {
        setFormError('Jadwal tidak ditemukan. Silakan coba lagi.')
        return
      }

      const updatedItem: AgendaItem = {
        ...oldItem,
        title: formTitle.trim(),
        time: formTime,
        category: formCategory,
        color,
      }

      if (editingAgenda.date === formDate) {
        updateAgenda(editingAgenda.date, editingAgenda.id, updatedItem)
      } else {
        deleteAgenda(editingAgenda.date, editingAgenda.id)
        addAgenda(formDate, updatedItem)
      }
    } else {
      const newItem: AgendaItem = {
        id: Date.now(),
        title: formTitle.trim(),
        time: formTime,
        category: formCategory,
        color,
        done: false,
      }

      addAgenda(formDate, newItem)
    }

    setSelectedDate(formDate)
    setDisplayMonth(new Date(`${formDate}T00:00:00`))
    setView('Bulan')
    closeForm()
  }

  function confirmDelete() {
    if (!deleteTarget) return

    deleteAgenda(deleteTarget.date, deleteTarget.id)
    setDeleteTarget(null)
  }

  const mondayOffset = (selected.getDay() + 6) % 7

  const weekStart = new Date(selected)
  weekStart.setDate(selected.getDate() - mondayOffset)

    date.setDate(weekStart.getDate() + index)

      ...Object.keys(agenda).filter(

      ),
      ...days
        .filter((day) => day.key >= todayKey)
        .filter((day) => getIndonesiaEvents(day.date).length > 0)
        .map((day) => day.key),
    ]),
  ).sort()

  const completedAgendaCount = selectedAgenda.filter(
    (item) => item.done,
  ).length

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-24">
      {/* Header kalender (ukuran font seragam dengan Hari Ini) */}
      <section className="space-y-3.5">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[25px] font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight">

          </p>

          <div className="flex justify-start">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setDisplayMonth(
                  new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1),
                )
                setSelectedDate(todayKey)
                setAgendaActionMode('normal')
              }}
              className="rounded-lg border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition hover:bg-[#ffdf7e] active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
            >
              Hari Ini

          </div>

          <button

            aria-label="Pilih bulan dan tahun"

                }`}

          })}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Kalender bulanan dan pekanan */}
      {view !== 'Agenda' && (
        <section className="rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-3 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
          <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 border-b border-[#dfd7cb] bg-[#f5f0e7]">
              {weekdayNames.map((day, index) => (
                <div

                    index === 6 ? 'bg-[#fcebea] text-[#ba1a1a]' : 'text-[#514b43]'

                >
                  {day}
                </div>
              ))}

            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 bg-white">

                const active = selectedDate === day.key
                const items = agenda[day.key] ?? []
                const nationalEvents = getIndonesiaEvents(day.date)

                const hasHoliday = nationalEvents.some(
                  (event) => event.type === 'libur',
                )

                const hasLeave = nationalEvents.some(
                  (event) => event.type === 'cuti',
                )

                const hasObservance = nationalEvents.some(
                  (event) => event.type === 'peringatan',
                )

                const dateColor = hasHoliday
                  ? 'text-[#ba1a1a]'
                  : hasLeave
                    ? 'text-[#d97706]'
                    : hasObservance
                      ? 'text-[#3976b9]'
                      : day.date.getDay() === 0
                        ? 'text-[#ba1a1a]'
                        : ''

                const isLastColumn = (index + 1) % 7 === 0
                const isLastRow = index >= visibleDays.length - 7

                return (
                  <button
                    key={day.key}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => selectDay(day.key)}
                    aria-label={`${formatLongDate(day.date)}${
                      nationalEvents.length
                        ? `, ${nationalEvents.map((event) => event.name).join(', ')}`
                        : ''
                    }`}
                    className={`flex min-h-[54px] flex-col items-center justify-center py-1.5 transition ${
                      !isLastColumn ? 'border-r border-[#e8e1d5]' : ''
                    } ${!isLastRow ? 'border-b border-[#e8e1d5]' : ''} ${
                      active
                        ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] font-extrabold text-[#252525]'
                        : day.today
                          ? 'bg-[#ede6ff] font-bold'
                          : 'bg-white hover:bg-[#faf5ec]'
                    } ${
                      !day.currentMonth && view === 'Bulan'
                        ? 'bg-[#faf7f2] opacity-35'
                        : ''
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className={`text-xs ${dateColor}`}>
                      {day.number}
                    </span>

                    <span className="mt-1 flex h-2 items-center gap-0.5">
                      {hasHoliday && (
                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#ba1a1a]" />
                      )}

                      {hasLeave && (
                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#d97706]" />
                      )}

                      {hasObservance && (
                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#3976b9]" />
                      )}

                      {items.slice(0, 2).map((item) => (
                        <span
                          key={item.id}
                          className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full border border-[#252525]/30"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: item.color }}
                        />
                      ))}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-2 border-t border-[#eee8df] pt-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-[#ba1a1a]" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#77716a]">
                Libur nasional
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-[#d97706]" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#77716a]">
                Cuti bersama
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-[#3976b9]" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#77716a]">
                Hari peringatan
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-[#b19ae9]" />
              <span className="text-[10px] text-[#77716a]">
                Aktivitas
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Tampilan agenda */}
      {view === 'Agenda' && (
        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold">
            Agenda dan Hari Nasional Mendatang
          </h2>

          {agendaDates.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#c7beb0] bg-white p-5 text-center text-xs text-[#77716a]">
              Belum ada agenda atau hari nasional mendatang.
            </p>
          ) : (
            agendaDates.map((key) => {
              const date = new Date(`${key}T00:00:00`)
              const events = getIndonesiaEvents(date)
              const items = agenda[key] ?? []

              return (
                <button
                  key={key}
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSelectedDate(key)
                    setDisplayMonth(
                      new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), 1),
                    )
                    setView('Bulan')
                  }}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-4 text-left shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525] transition hover:bg-[#fffaf1]"
                >
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold">
                    {formatLongDate(date)}
                  </p>

                  {events.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
                      {events.map((event, index) => (
                        <div
                          key={`${event.name}-${index}`}
                          className="flex items-center gap-2"
                        >
                          <span
                            className="h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                            style={{
                              backgroundColor: getEventColor(event.type),
                            }}
                          />
                          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f493f]">
                            {event.name}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {items.length > 0 && (
                    <span className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#14532d]">
                      {items.length} aktivitas
                    </span>
                  )}
                </button>
              )
            })
          )}
        </section>
      )}

      {/* Detail agenda tanggal terpilih:
1. Tanggal -> 2. Aktivitas terselesaikan -> 3. CRUD terpusat di tengah -> 4. Output jadwal */}
      <section className="space-y-3.5">
        {/* 1. Baris Tanggal */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold">
            {formatLongDate(selected)}
          </h2>

          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-[#FB923C] px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
            {selectedAgenda.filter((item) => !item.done).length} sisa
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* 2. Progress Aktivitas Terselesaikan */}
        <div className="rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#f1ede6] p-3 shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
              <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80]" />
              Aktivitas terselesaikan
            </span>
            <span className="rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#14532d]">
              {completedAgendaCount}/{selectedAgenda.length}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-2 h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-white">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full bg-[#4ADE80] transition-all"
              style={{
                width: selectedAgenda.length
                  ? `${
                      (completedAgendaCount / selectedAgenda.length) * 100
                    }%`
                  : '0%',
              }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* 3. Baris CRUD di Tengah: Tambah Jadwal (~50% lebar, Ungu Muda) + Ikon Edit + Ikon Hapus */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 pt-0.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={openAddForm}
            className="flex h-9 w-44 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#D9CCFF] px-3 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition hover:bg-[#cfc0ff] active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} />
            <span>Tambah Jadwal</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              setAgendaActionMode((current) =>
                current === 'edit' ? 'normal' : 'edit',
              )
            }
            aria-label="Edit jadwal"
            aria-pressed={agendaActionMode === 'edit'}
            title="Edit jadwal"
            className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] text-[#252525] transition active:translate-x-[2px] active:translate-y-[2px] active:shadow-none ${
              agendaActionMode === 'edit'
                ? 'translate-x-[2px] translate-y-[2px] bg-[#f7d874] shadow-none'
                : 'bg-[#ffe69a] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
            }`}
          >
            <Pencil size={14} />
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() =>
              setAgendaActionMode((current) =>
                current === 'delete' ? 'normal' : 'delete',
              )
            }
            aria-label="Hapus jadwal"
            aria-pressed={agendaActionMode === 'delete'}
            title="Hapus jadwal"
            className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] text-[#252525] transition active:translate-x-[2px] active:translate-y-[2px] active:shadow-none ${
              agendaActionMode === 'delete'
                ? 'translate-x-[2px] translate-y-[2px] bg-[#ff9e8d] shadow-none'
                : 'bg-[#ffb5a7] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
            }`}
          >
            <Trash2 size={14} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {agendaActionMode !== 'normal' && (
          <p className="rounded-lg bg-[#f5f0e7] px-3 py-2 text-center text-xs font-medium text-[#77716a]">
            {agendaActionMode === 'edit'
              ? 'Pilih jadwal di bawah yang ingin diedit.'
              : 'Pilih jadwal di bawah yang ingin dihapus.'}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAgendaActionMode('normal')}
              className="ml-2 font-bold text-[#252525] underline"
            >
              Batal
            </button>
          </p>
        )}

        {/* 4. Hari Nasional & Output dari Tambah Jadwal */}
        <NationalEventList date={selected} />

        {selectedAgenda.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#c7beb0] bg-white px-4 py-9 text-center">
            <CalendarDays size={28} className="mx-auto text-[#aaa197]" />
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm font-bold">
              Belum ada jadwal
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#77716a]">
              Hari ini masih bisa kamu isi dengan hal produktif.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {selectedAgenda
              .slice()
              .sort((a, b) => a.time.localeCompare(b.time))
              .map((item) => (
                <div
                  key={item.id}
                  role="button"
                  tabIndex={0}
                  onClick={() => handleAgendaCardClick(item)}
                  onKeyDown={(event) => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
                      event.preventDefault()
                      handleAgendaCardClick(item)
                    }
                  }}
                  className={`cursor-pointer select-none rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] p-3.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525] transition-all active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none ${
                    agendaActionMode === 'edit'
                      ? 'bg-[#fff3c4]'
                      : agendaActionMode === 'delete'
                        ? 'bg-[#ffe0d9]'
                        : item.done
                          ? 'bg-[#BDECCF]'
                          : 'bg-white'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <span
                      aria-hidden="true"
                      className={`mt-0.5 flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] ${
                        agendaActionMode === 'edit'
                          ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525]'
                          : agendaActionMode === 'delete'
                            ? 'bg-[#ffb5a7] text-[#252525]'
                            : item.done
                              ? 'bg-[#252525] text-white'
                              : 'bg-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {agendaActionMode === 'edit' ? (
                        <Pencil size={12} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                      ) : agendaActionMode === 'delete' ? (
                        <Trash2 size={12} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                      ) : (
                        item.done && <Check size={13} strokeWidth={2.8} />
                      )}
                    </span>

                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                        <h3
                          className={`text-sm font-bold leading-snug ${
                            item.done ? 'line-through text-[#1e4620]' : ''
                          }`}
                        >
                          {item.title}
                        </h3>

                        <span
                          className="shrink-0 rounded border border-[#252525]/20 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold"
                          style={{
                            backgroundColor: item.done
                              ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)'
                              : `${item.color}88`,
                          }}
                        >
                          {item.done ? 'Selesai' : 'Aktif'}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div
                        className={`mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold ${
                          item.done ? 'text-[#1e4620]' : 'text-[#77716a]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Clock3 size={12} />
                        {item.time} WIB
                        <span>â€¢</span>
                        {item.category}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {/* Modal Pemilih Bulan & Tahun */}
      {showMonthPicker &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            onClick={() => setShowMonthPicker(false)}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[75] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#252525]/40 px-4 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-label="Pilih bulan dan tahun"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
              className="w-full max-w-xs rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-4 shadow-[5px_5px_0_#252525]"
            >
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-sm font-extrabold">
                  Pilih Bulan &amp; Tahun
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowMonthPicker(false)}
                  aria-label="Tutup pemilih bulan"
                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#f1ede6] p-1.5 hover:bg-[#e5dfd3]"
                >
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setPickerYear((y) => y - 1)}
                  aria-label="Tahun sebelumnya"
                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#f5f0e7] p-1.5 hover:bg-[#ffe69a]"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft size={16} />
                </button>

                <span className="text-base font-extrabold">{pickerYear}</span>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setPickerYear((y) => y + 1)}
                  aria-label="Tahun berikutnya"
                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#f5f0e7] p-1.5 hover:bg-[#ffe69a]"
                >
                  <ChevronRight size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                {monthNames.map((month, index) => {
                  const isCurrentSelection =
                    displayMonth.getMonth() === index &&
                    displayMonth.getFullYear() === pickerYear

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={month}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => jumpToMonth(index)}
                      className={`rounded-xl border-[1.5px] py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                        isCurrentSelection
                          ? 'border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                          : 'border-[#e3dbcf] bg-white hover:border-[#252525] hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {month.slice(0, 3)}
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}

      {/* Modal tambah dan edit jadwal */}
      {showForm &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#252525]/40 px-4 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
            onClick={(event) => {
              if (event.target === event.currentTarget) {
                closeForm()
              }
            }}
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-md overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 shadow-[5px_5px_0_#252525]"
            >
              <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">
                    {editingAgenda ? 'Edit Jadwal' : 'Tambah Jadwal'}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#77716a]">
                    {editingAgenda
                      ? 'Perbarui informasi kegiatanmu.'
                      : 'Catat kegiatan yang ingin kamu lakukan.'}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={closeForm}
                  aria-label="Tutup formulir"
                  className="rounded-lg bg-[#f1ede6] p-2 hover:bg-[#e5dfd3]"
                >
                  <X size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                    Nama kegiatan
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    autoFocus
                    rows={1}
                    enterKeyHint="done"
                    value={formTitle}
                    onChange={(event) => {
                      setFormTitle(event.target.value.replace(/\r?\n/g, ''))
                      setFormError('')
                    }}
                    onKeyDown={(event) => {
                      if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                        event.preventDefault()
                        event.currentTarget.blur()
                      }
                    }}
                    placeholder="Contoh: Mengerjakan tugas kuliah"
                    className="w-full resize-none overflow-x-auto whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-3 text-sm text-[#252525] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#9c9489] focus:bg-[#fffdf7] focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                      Tanggal
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="date"
                      value={formDate}
                      onChange={(event) => setFormDate(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full min-w-0 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-3 text-xs font-semibold outline-none focus:bg-[#ffe69a]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                      Waktu
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="time"
                      value={formTime}
                      onChange={(event) => setFormTime(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-3 text-xs font-semibold outline-none focus:bg-[#ffe69a]"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                    Kategori
                  </label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                    {categories.map((category) => (
                      <button
                        key={category.name}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setFormCategory(category.name)}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition ${
                          formCategory === category.name
                            ? 'border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                            : 'border-[#ddd4c7] bg-white hover:border-[#252525]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span
                          className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full border border-[#252525]"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: category.color }}
                        />
                        {category.name}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {formError && (
                  <p className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-red-600">
                    {formError}
                  </p>
                )}

                <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={closeForm}
                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]"
                  >
                    Batal
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={saveSchedule}
                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#D9CCFF] px-4 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#252525] shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
                  >
                    {editingAgenda ? 'Simpan Perubahan' : 'Simpan Jadwal'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}

      {/* Modal konfirmasi hapus */}
      {deleteTarget &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[80] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#252525]/40 px-4 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
              className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 shadow-[5px_5px_0_#252525]"
            >
              <div className="mx-auto flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-[#FFB5A7]/40 text-[#252525]">
                <AlertTriangle size={23} />
              </div>

              <h2 className="mt-4 text-center text-base font-bold">
                Hapus jadwal ini?
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-2 text-center text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#77716a]">
                Jadwal <strong className="text-[#252525]">{deleteTarget.title}</strong> akan dihapus secara permanen dari kalender.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-5 flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={confirmDelete}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#FFB5A7] px-4 py-3 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]"
                >
                  Ya, Hapus
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}
    </div>
  )
}

import {
  useEffect,
  useId,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ElementType,
  type ChangeEvent,
} from 'react'

import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'

import {
  Languages,
  CalendarDays,
  Clock3,
  Sunrise,
  AlertCircle,
  Timer,
  Moon,
  Heart,
  Target,
  Coffee,
  Database,
  Download,
  Upload,

type DefaultToneId = 'tone-1' | 'tone-2' | 'tone-3'
type SelectedToneId = DefaultToneId | 'custom'

const defaultAlarmTones: {
  id: DefaultToneId
  name: string
  src: string
}[] = [
  {
    id: 'tone-1',
    name: 'Your Phone Linging',
    src: `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}audio/the-phone-lingering.mp3`,
  },

    name: '#jedagjedug - Zachz Winner',

  },
]

function MarqueeText({
  text,
  className = '',
}: {
  text: string
  className?: string
}) {
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const textRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null)
  const rawId = useId()
  const animName = `marquee-${rawId.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '')}`

  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<{
    overflow: boolean
    shift: number
    totalDuration: number
    pausePercent: number
  }>({
    overflow: false,
    shift: 0,
    totalDuration: 0,
    pausePercent: 0,
  })

  const gap = 36

  useEffect(() => {
    const container = containerRef.current
    const textEl = textRef.current
    if (!container || !textEl) return

    const measure = () => {
      const containerWidth = container.clientWidth
      const textWidth = textEl.scrollWidth

      if (containerWidth > 0 && textWidth > containerWidth + 1) {
        const shift = textWidth + gap
        const pauseSeconds = 2
        const scrollSeconds = Math.max(3, shift / 38)
        const totalDuration = pauseSeconds + scrollSeconds
        const pausePercent = Number(
          ((pauseSeconds / totalDuration) * 100).toFixed(2),
        )

        setMetrics({
          overflow: true,
          shift,
          totalDuration,
          pausePercent,
        })
      } else {
        setMetrics((prev) =>
          prev.overflow
            ? { overflow: false, shift: 0, totalDuration: 0, pausePercent: 0 }
            : prev,
        )
      }
    }

    measure()

    const observer = new ResizeObserver(measure)
    observer.observe(container)
    observer.observe(textEl)

    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [text])

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className={`min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden ${className}`}
    >
      {metrics.overflow && (
        <style>{`
          @keyframes ${animName} {
            0%, ${metrics.pausePercent}% {
              transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
            }
            100% {
              transform: translate3d(-${metrics.shift}px, 0, 0);
            }
          }
        `}</style>
      )}

      <div
        className="inline-flex whitespace-nowrap will-change-transform"
        style={
          metrics.overflow
            ? {
                animation: `${animName} ${metrics.totalDuration.toFixed(2)}s linear infinite`,
              }
            : undefined
        }
      >
        <span ref={textRef} className="inline-block whitespace-nowrap">
          {text}
        </span>

        {metrics.overflow && (
          <span
            aria-hidden="true"
            className="inline-block whitespace-nowrap"
            style={{ paddingLeft: `${gap}px` }}
          >
            {text}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

type SettingItemProps = {
  icon: ElementType
  title: string
  description: string
  value?: string
  onClick?: () => void
}

function SettingItem({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  description,
  value,
  onClick,
}: SettingItemProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl px-2 py-3 text-left transition hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]"
    >
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#eee8df]">
          <Icon size={18} />
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">{title}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#888078]">{description}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
        {value && (
          <span className="rounded-lg bg-[#eee8df] px-2 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold">
            {value}
          </span>
        )}
        <ChevronRight size={17} className="text-[#999188]" />
      </div>
    </button>
  )
}

type ToggleItemProps = {
  icon: ElementType
  title: string
  description: string
  checked: boolean
  onChange: () => void
}

function ToggleItem({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  description,
  checked,
  onChange,
}: ToggleItemProps) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-2 py-3">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#eee8df]">
          <Icon size={18} />
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">{title}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#888078]">{description}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <button
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        aria-checked={checked}
        onClick={onChange}
        className={`relative h-6 w-12 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] p-0.5 shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525] transition ${
          checked ? 'bg-[#4ADE80]' : 'bg-[#EF4444]'
        }`}
      >
        <span
          className={`block h-4 w-4 rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-white transition-transform ${
            checked ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-0'
          }`}
        />
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

type OptionKey = 'focus' | 'break' | 'target'

type InfoKey =
  | 'language'
  | 'calendar'
  | 'time'
  | 'version'
  | 'privacy'
  | 'cache'
  | 'feedback'

type MoreProps = {
  onEditProfile: () => void
  scrollToNotificationsTick?: number
}

const infoContent: Record<
  InfoKey,
  { title: string; description: string; icon: ElementType }
> = {
  language: {
    title: 'Bahasa Antarmuka',
    description:
      'Bismillah Kelar saat ini menggunakan Bahasa Indonesia sebagai bahasa utama. Dukungan bahasa lainnya dapat ditambahkan pada pengembangan berikutnya.',
    icon: Languages,

    icon: MessageSquare,
  },
}

function isValidBackup(value: unknown): value is AppData {
  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object') return false

  const backup = value as Record<string, unknown>

  if (!Array.isArray(backup.tasks)) return false
  if (!backup.agenda || typeof backup.agenda !== 'object') return false
  if (!backup.settings || typeof backup.settings !== 'object') return false

  const settings = backup.settings as Record<string, unknown>

  if (!settings.notifications || typeof settings.notifications !== 'object') {
    return false
  }

  if (
    typeof settings.focusDuration !== 'string' ||
    typeof settings.breakDuration !== 'string' ||
    typeof settings.dailyTarget !== 'string' ||
    typeof settings.greeting !== 'string'
  ) {
    return false
  }

  const validTasks = backup.tasks.every((task) => {
    if (!task || typeof task !== 'object') return false

    const item = task as Record<string, unknown>

    return (
      typeof item.id === 'number' &&
      typeof item.title === 'string' &&
      typeof item.category === 'string' &&
      typeof item.priority === 'string' &&
      typeof item.date === 'string' &&
      typeof item.time === 'string' &&
      typeof item.status === 'string'
    )
  })

  if (!validTasks) return false

  const agenda = backup.agenda as Record<string, unknown>

  const validAgenda = Object.values(agenda).every((items) => {
    if (!Array.isArray(items)) return false

    return items.every((item) => {
      if (!item || typeof item !== 'object') return false

      const entry = item as Record<string, unknown>

      return (
        typeof entry.id === 'number' &&
        typeof entry.title === 'string' &&
        typeof entry.time === 'string' &&
        typeof entry.category === 'string' &&
        typeof entry.color === 'string' &&
        typeof entry.done === 'boolean'
      )
    })
  })

  return validAgenda
}

export default function More({
  onEditProfile,
  scrollToNotificationsTick = 0,
}: MoreProps) {
  const {
    data,
    profilePhotoUrl,
    updateSettings,
    replaceData,
  } = useAppData()

  const profileName = data.profile.name.trim() || 'Teman'
  const avatarSrc = profilePhotoUrl || DEFAULT_PROFILE_PHOTO_URL

  const {
    notifications,
    focusDuration,
    breakDuration,
    dailyTarget,
    greeting,
    soundVolume = 1,
  } = data.settings

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const previewAudioRef = useRef<HTMLAudioElement | null>(null)
  const notificationsSectionRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null)

  const [activeOption, setActiveOption] = useState<OptionKey | null>(null)
  const [draftPreset, setDraftPreset] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [customValue, setCustomValue] = useState('')
  const [customError, setCustomError] = useState('')

  const [activeInfo, setActiveInfo] = useState<InfoKey | null>(null)
  const [feedbackText, setFeedbackText] = useState('')
  const [showLocalStorageDetail, setShowLocalStorageDetail] = useState(false)
  const [showPrayerEasterEgg, setShowPrayerEasterEgg] = useState(false)

  const [showRestoreConfirm, setShowRestoreConfirm] = useState(false)
  const [pendingBackup, setPendingBackup] = useState<AppData | null>(null)

  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<{
    type: 'success' | 'error'
    message: string
  } | null>(null)

  const [selectedTone, setSelectedTone] = useState<SelectedToneId>(
    () => {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY)
      if (
        saved === 'tone-2' ||
        saved === 'tone-3' ||
        saved === 'custom'
      ) {
        return saved
      }
      return 'tone-1'
    },
  )

  const [soundName, setSoundName] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [soundLoading, setSoundLoading] = useState(true)
  const [soundProcessing, setSoundProcessing] = useState(false)
  const [isPreviewing, setIsPreviewing] = useState(false)
  const isPreviewingRef = useRef(false)

  const [interactionSound, setInteractionSound] = useState(
    () => localStorage.getItem(INTERACTION_SOUND_KEY) !== 'false',
  )

  // Animasi scroll otomatis dari kartu profil atas ke tengah bagian Notifikasi & Pengingat
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!scrollToNotificationsTick) return

    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'auto' })

    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      notificationsSectionRef.current?.scrollIntoView({
        behavior: 'smooth',
        block: 'center',
      })
    }, 320)

    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [scrollToNotificationsTick])

  const showNotice = (
    message: string,
    type: 'success' | 'error' = 'success',
  ) => {
    setNotice({ message, type })
  }

  const stopDefaultPreview = () => {
    if (previewAudioRef.current) {
      previewAudioRef.current.pause()
      previewAudioRef.current.currentTime = 0
      previewAudioRef.current = null
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true

    async function loadSound() {
      try {
        const sound = await getTimerSound()

        if (active) {
          const loadedName = sound?.name ?? null
          setSoundName(loadedName)

          if (!loadedName && localStorage.getItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY) === 'custom') {
            setSelectedTone('tone-1')
            localStorage.setItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY, 'tone-1')
          }
        }
      } catch {
        if (active) {
          setNotice({
            message: 'Gagal membaca penyimpanan audio.',
            type: 'error',
          })
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) setSoundLoading(false)
      }
    }

    void loadSound()

    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    isPreviewingRef.current = isPreviewing
  }, [isPreviewing])

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (isPreviewingRef.current) {
        stopTimerEndSound()
        stopDefaultPreview()
      }
    }
  }, [])

  const toggleInteractionSound = () => {
    const nextValue = !interactionSound

    setInteractionSound(nextValue)

    localStorage.setItem(
      INTERACTION_SOUND_KEY,
      String(nextValue),
    )

    showNotice(
      nextValue
        ? 'Efek suara interaksi diaktifkan.'
        : 'Efek suara interaksi dinonaktifkan.',
    )
  }

  const optionSettings: Record<
    OptionKey,
    {
      title: string
      description: string
      current: string
      options: string[]
      unit: string
      label: string
      min: number
      max: number
      save: (value: string) => void
    }
  > = {
    focus: {
      title: 'Durasi Standar Fokus',
      description: 'Pilih durasi untuk satu sesi fokus.',
      current: focusDuration,
      options: [
        '15 Menit',
        '25 Menit',
        '30 Menit',
        '45 Menit',
        '60 Menit',
        '90 Menit',
      ],
      unit: 'Menit',
      label: 'Durasi fokus',
      min: 1,
      max: 1440,
      save: (value) => updateSettings({ focusDuration: value }),
    },
    break: {
      title: 'Istirahat Singkat',
      description: 'Pilih jeda antar sesi fokus.',
      current: breakDuration,
      options: ['5 Menit', '10 Menit', '15 Menit', '20 Menit'],
      unit: 'Menit',
      label: 'Durasi istirahat',
      min: 1,
      max: 1440,
      save: (value) => updateSettings({ breakDuration: value }),
    },
    target: {
      title: 'Target Harian',
      description: 'Tentukan jumlah target yang ingin diselesaikan.',
      current: dailyTarget,
      options: [
        '1 - 2 Target',
        '3 - 5 Target',
        '6 - 8 Target',
        '9 - 10 Target',
      ],
      unit: 'Target',
      label: 'Jumlah target harian',
      min: 1,
      max: 100,
      save: (value) => updateSettings({ dailyTarget: value }),
    },
  }

  const selectedOption = activeOption
    ? optionSettings[activeOption]
    : null

  const openOption = (key: OptionKey) => {
    const setting = optionSettings[key]
    const isPreset = setting.options.includes(setting.current)

    setDraftPreset(isPreset ? setting.current : null)
    setCustomValue(
      isPreset ? '' : (setting.current.match(/\d+/)?.[0] ?? ''),
    )
    setCustomError('')
    setActiveOption(key)
  }

  const toggleNotification = (
    key: keyof typeof notifications,
  ) => {
    updateSettings({
      notifications: {
        ...notifications,
        [key]: !notifications[key],
      },
    })
  }

  const saveCustomOption = () => {
    if (!selectedOption) return

    if (draftPreset) {
      selectedOption.save(draftPreset)
      setActiveOption(null)
      setDraftPreset(null)
      setCustomError('')
      showNotice('Personalisasi berhasil disimpan.')
      return
    }

    const value = Number(customValue)

    if (
      !customValue.trim() ||
      !Number.isInteger(value) ||
      value < selectedOption.min ||
      value > selectedOption.max
    ) {
      setCustomError(
        `Masukkan angka bulat antara ${selectedOption.min}â€“${selectedOption.max}.`,
      )
      return
    }

    selectedOption.save(`${value} ${selectedOption.unit}`)
    setActiveOption(null)
    setDraftPreset(null)
    setCustomError('')
    showNotice('Personalisasi berhasil disimpan.')
  }

  const selectTone = (toneId: SelectedToneId) => {
    stopTimerEndSound()
    stopDefaultPreview()
    setIsPreviewing(false)

    setSelectedTone(toneId)
    localStorage.setItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY, toneId)

    if (toneId === 'custom') {
      showNotice(`Nada alarm diubah ke ${soundName ?? 'MP3 kustom'}.`)
    } else {
      const chosen = defaultAlarmTones.find((tone) => tone.id === toneId)
      showNotice(`Nada alarm diubah ke ${chosen?.name ?? 'nada default'}.`)
    }
  }

  const handleSoundFile = async (
    event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
  ) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0]

    if (!file) return

    const isMp3 =
      file.type === 'audio/mpeg' ||
      file.type === 'audio/mp3' ||
      file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.mp3')

    if (!isMp3) {
      showNotice('Format audio harus berupa file MP3.', 'error')
      event.target.value = ''
      return
    }

    if (file.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) {
      showNotice('Ukuran MP3 maksimal 15 MB.', 'error')
      event.target.value = ''
      return
    }

    setSoundProcessing(true)

    try {
      stopTimerEndSound()
      stopDefaultPreview()
      setIsPreviewing(false)

      await saveTimerSound(file)
      setSoundName(file.name)
      setSelectedTone('custom')
      localStorage.setItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY, 'custom')
      showNotice('Suara timer berhasil disimpan.')
    } catch {
      showNotice('Gagal menyimpan file MP3.', 'error')
    } finally {
      setSoundProcessing(false)
      event.target.value = ''
    }
  }

  const playDefaultTonePreview = async (toneId: DefaultToneId) => {
    stopDefaultPreview()

    const tone =
      defaultAlarmTones.find((item) => item.id === toneId) ??
      defaultAlarmTones[0]

    const audio = new Audio(tone.src)
    audio.volume = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, soundVolume))
    previewAudioRef.current = audio

    audio.onended = () => {
      setIsPreviewing(false)
      previewAudioRef.current = null
    }

    try {
      await audio.play()
      setIsPreviewing(true)
      showNotice(`Memutar preview: ${tone.name}`)
    } catch {
      setIsPreviewing(false)
      previewAudioRef.current = null
      showNotice('Preview audio gagal diputar.', 'error')
    }
  }

  const previewSound = async () => {
    if (isPreviewing) {
      stopTimerEndSound()
      stopDefaultPreview()
      setIsPreviewing(false)
      return
    }

    if (selectedTone !== 'custom' || !soundName) {
      const toneToPlay: DefaultToneId =
        selectedTone === 'custom' ? 'tone-1' : selectedTone
      await playDefaultTonePreview(toneToPlay)
      return
    }

    try {
      const started = await playTimerEndSound(
        soundVolume,
        setIsPreviewing,
      )

      if (!started) {
        setIsPreviewing(false)
        showNotice('Preview audio tidak dapat diputar.', 'error')
        return
      }

      showNotice(`Memutar preview: ${soundName}`)
    } catch {
      setIsPreviewing(false)
      showNotice('Preview audio gagal diputar.', 'error')
    }
  }

  const removeSound = async () => {
    setSoundProcessing(true)

    try {
      stopTimerEndSound()
      stopDefaultPreview()
      setIsPreviewing(false)

      await deleteTimerSound()
      setSoundName(null)
      if (selectedTone === 'custom') {
        setSelectedTone('tone-1')
        localStorage.setItem(DEFAULT_ALARM_TONE_KEY, 'tone-1')
      }
      showNotice('MP3 dihapus. Nada bawaan aktif kembali.')
    } catch {
      showNotice('Gagal menghapus file MP3.', 'error')
    } finally {
      setSoundProcessing(false)
    }
  }

  const handleVolumeChange = (value: number) => {
    if (previewAudioRef.current) {
      previewAudioRef.current.volume = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, value))
    }

    updateSettings({
      soundVolume: value,
    })
  }

  const exportData = () => {
    try {
      const fileContent = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)
      const blob = new Blob([fileContent], {
        type: 'application/json',
      })

      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
      const link = document.createElement('a')

      link.href = url
      link.download = `bismillah-kelar-${new Date()
        .toISOString()
        .slice(0, 10)}.json`

      document.body.appendChild(link)
      link.click()
      document.body.removeChild(link)

      URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
      showNotice('Backup data berhasil diunduh.')
    } catch {
      showNotice('Gagal mengekspor data.', 'error')
    }
  }

  const handleBackupFile = async (
    event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
  ) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0]

    if (!file) return

    try {
      const content = await file.text()
      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(content)

      if (!isValidBackup(parsed)) {
        showNotice(
          'File tidak sesuai dengan format backup Bismillah Kelar.',
          'error',
        )
        return
      }

      setPendingBackup(parsed)
      setShowRestoreConfirm(true)
    } catch {
      showNotice(
        'File tidak dapat dibaca. Pastikan file JSON tidak rusak.',
        'error',
      )
    } finally {
      event.target.value = ''
    }
  }

  const restoreBackup = () => {
    if (!pendingBackup) return

    replaceData(pendingBackup)
    setPendingBackup(null)
    setShowRestoreConfirm(false)
    showNotice('Data berhasil dipulihkan dari backup.')
  }

  const sendFeedback = () => {
    const message = feedbackText.trim()

    if (!message) return

    const recipient = 'zetzayn@students.unnes.ac.id'
    const subject = encodeURIComponent(
      'Umpan Balik Bismillah Kelar',
    )
    const body = encodeURIComponent(message)

    window.location.href =
      `mailto:${recipient}?subject=${subject}&body=${body}`

    setActiveInfo(null)
    setFeedbackText('')
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-7 pb-5">
      {notice && (
        <div
          className={`flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border p-3 text-sm ${
            notice.type === 'success'
              ? 'border-[#7eae8d] bg-[#e5f5e9] text-[#385a47]'
              : 'border-[#d99a8c] bg-[#fff0ec] text-[#8b4436]'
          }`}
        >
          {notice.type === 'success' ? (
            <Check size={17} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          ) : (
            <AlertCircle size={17} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          )}
          <p className="flex-1">{notice.message}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setNotice(null)}
            aria-label="Tutup pemberitahuan"
          >
            <X size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KARTU PROFIL ATAS */}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onEditProfile}
        aria-label="Edit profil dan sapaan beranda"
        className="block w-full rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#D9CCFF] p-4 text-left shadow-[3px_3px_0px_#252525] transition hover:brightness-[0.98] active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
          <div className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1]">
            <img
              src={avatarSrc}
              alt={`Foto profil ${profileName}`}
              className="h-full w-full object-cover"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <h2 className="truncate text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-[#252525]">
              {profileName}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-[#51476b]">
              Ketuk untuk edit profil &amp; sapaan
            </p>
          </div>

          <span className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#252525] bg-white/85">
            <Pencil size={15} />
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-[#252525]/20 bg-white/75 px-3 py-2.5">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-[#51476b]">
            Sapaan beranda
          </p>
          <p className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
            {greeting}
          </p>
        </div>
      </button>

      {/* PREFERENCES */}
      <section>
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
          Preferensi aplikasi
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-[#eee8df]">
          <SettingItem
            icon={Languages}
            title="Bahasa Antarmuka"
            description="Bahasa yang digunakan aplikasi"
            value="Indonesia"
            onClick={() => setActiveInfo('language')}
          />

          <SettingItem
            icon={CalendarDays}
            title="Hari Awal Pekan"
            description="Orientasi kalender mingguan"
            value="Senin"

          />

          <SettingItem
            icon={Clock3}
            title="Format Waktu"

            value="24 Jam WIB"
            onClick={() => setActiveInfo('time')}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* NOTIFICATIONS (Target Auto-Scroll ke Tengah Layar) */}
      <section ref={notificationsSectionRef} className="scroll-my-24">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
            Notifikasi &amp; Pengingat
          </h3>
        </div>

        <div className="divide-y divide-[#eee8df]">
          <ToggleItem
            icon={Sunrise}
            title="Pengingat Target Harian"
            description="Pukul 07:00 WIB"
            checked={notifications.daily}
            onChange={() => toggleNotification('daily')}
          />

          <ToggleItem
            icon={AlertCircle}
            title="Batas Waktu & Deadline"
            description="2 jam sebelum tenggat"
            checked={notifications.deadline}
            onChange={() => toggleNotification('deadline')}
          />

          <ToggleItem
            icon={Timer}
            title="Sesi Fokus (Pomodoro)"
            description="Pengingat sesi fokus dan istirahat"
            checked={notifications.focus}
            onChange={() => toggleNotification('focus')}
          />

          <ToggleItem
            icon={Moon}
            title="Refleksi Malam Hari"
            description="Pukul 21:30 WIB"
            checked={notifications.reflection}
            onChange={() => toggleNotification('reflection')}
          />

          <ToggleItem
            icon={Heart}
            title="Pengingat Kebiasaan"
            description="Mengaji, olahraga, tidur 8 jam, dan kebiasaan lainnya"
            checked={notifications.habits}
            onChange={() => toggleNotification('habits')}
          />
        </div>

        <p className="mt-2 rounded-xl bg-[#fff2d5] p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#77664a]">
          Preferensi pengingat tersimpan di perangkat dan tampil di ikon lonceng header.
        </p>
      </section>

      {/* FOCUS SETTINGS */}
      <section>
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
          Personalisasi Fokus &amp; Target
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-[#eee8df]">
          <SettingItem
            icon={Target}

            onClick={() => openOption('focus')}
          />

          <SettingItem
            icon={Coffee}
            title="Istirahat Singkat"

          <SettingItem
            icon={Target}

      </section>

      {/* NADA ALARM POMODORO */}
      <section>
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
          Nada Alarm Pomodoro
        </h3>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#D9CCFF] p-4 shadow-[3px_3px_0px_#252525]">

            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">

              <p className="text-xs text-[#625779]">

              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-[#252525]/20 bg-white/75 p-3">
