            </p>

            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              {defaultAlarmTones.map((tone) => {
                const isSelected = selectedTone === tone.id

                return (
                  <button
                    key={tone.id}
                    type="button"
                    disabled={soundProcessing || soundLoading}
                    onClick={() => selectTone(tone.id)}
                    className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2.5 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-bold transition ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] text-[#14532d] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                        : 'border-[#252525]/25 bg-white text-[#252525] hover:border-[#252525]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <MarqueeText text={tone.name} />
                    {isSelected && (
                      <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-white text-[#252525]">
                        <Check size={12} strokeWidth={2.8} />
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </button>
                )
              })}

              {soundName && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  disabled={soundProcessing || soundLoading}
                  onClick={() => selectTone('custom')}
                  className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-bold transition ${
                    selectedTone === 'custom'
                      ? 'border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] text-[#14532d] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                      : 'border-[#252525]/25 bg-white text-[#252525] hover:border-[#252525]'
                  }`}
                >
                  <MarqueeText text={soundName} />

                  <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5">
                    <span className="rounded-md border border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-extrabold text-[#14532d]">
                      MP3
                    </span>

                    {selectedTone === 'custom' && (
                      <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-white text-[#252525]">
                        <Check size={12} strokeWidth={2.8} />
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-[#252525]/20 bg-white/75 p-3">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Volume2 size={17} />
                <p className="text-xs font-extrabold">
                  Volume Suara Timer
                </p>
              </div>

              <span className="rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-extrabold tabular-nums text-[#14532d]">
                {Math.round(soundVolume * 100)}%
              </span>
            </div>

            <input
              type="range"
              min="0"
              max="1"
              step="0.05"
              value={soundVolume}
              onChange={(event) =>
                handleVolumeChange(Number(event.target.value))
              }
              style={{
                background: `linear-gradient(to right, #4ADE80 0%, #4ADE80 ${
                  soundVolume * 100
                }%, #e5dfd5 ${soundVolume * 100}%, #e5dfd5 100%)`,
              }}
              className="h-2.5 w-full cursor-pointer appearance-none rounded-full border border-[#252525] accent-[#4ADE80]"
              aria-label="Volume suara timer"
            />

            <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between text-[10px] text-[#777078]">
              <span>Diam</span>
              <span>50%</span>
              <span>Maksimal</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <input
            ref={fileInputRef}
            type="file"
            accept=".mp3,audio/mpeg,audio/mp3"
            className="hidden"
            onChange={handleSoundFile}
          />

          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={soundProcessing || soundLoading}
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525] transition active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {soundProcessing ? (
                <LoaderCircle size={16} className="animate-spin" />
              ) : (
                <Upload size={16} />
              )}
              {soundName ? 'Ganti MP3' : 'Impor MP3'}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={soundProcessing || soundLoading}
              onClick={previewSound}
              className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#14532d] shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525] transition hover:bg-[#3ad170] active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isPreviewing ? (
                <Square size={15} />
              ) : (
                <Play size={16} />
              )}
              {isPreviewing ? 'Stop Preview' : 'Preview'}
            </button>
          </div>

          {soundName && (
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={soundProcessing}
              onClick={removeSound}
              className="mt-3 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#EF4444] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-extrabold text-white shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition hover:bg-[#DC2626] active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <Trash2 size={15} />
              Hapus MP3
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* EFEK SUARA APLIKASI */}
      <section>
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
          Efek Suara Aplikasi
        </h3>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] p-3 shadow-[3px_3px_0px_#252525]">
          <ToggleItem
            icon={Volume2}
            title="Suara Interaksi"
            description="Efek suara saat klik menu, tombol, dan pilihan"
            checked={interactionSound}
            onChange={toggleInteractionSound}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* DATA & CADANGAN */}
      <section>
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
          Data &amp; Cadangan
        </h3>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setShowLocalStorageDetail((current) => !current)}
          aria-expanded={showLocalStorageDetail}
          className="w-full rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#BDECCF] p-4 text-left shadow-[3px_3px_0px_#252525] transition hover:brightness-[0.98] active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#1e4620]">
              <Database size={15} />
              Penyimpanan Lokal
            </span>

            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#1e4620]">
              {showLocalStorageDetail ? 'Tutup detail' : 'Cara kerja'}
              <ChevronDown
                size={15}
                className={`transition-transform ${
                  showLocalStorageDetail ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                }`}
              />
            </span>
          </div>

          <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#234d2c]">
            Data disimpan secara lokal pada perangkat ini melalui penyimpanan browser dan tetap bisa diakses penuh saat offline.
          </p>

          {showLocalStorageDetail && (
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-[#252525]/25 bg-white/85 p-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#252525]">
              <p>
                <strong>1. 100% Offline &amp; Privat:</strong> Target dan tugas, agenda kalender, progres, dan pengaturan tersimpan langsung di memori browser HP/laptop kamu tanpa dikirim ke server mana pun.
              </p>
              <p>
                <strong>2. Media &amp; Audio Lokal:</strong> Foto profil kustom dan file audio MP3 disimpan di database perangkat (IndexedDB) agar tetap responsif tanpa internet.
              </p>
              <p>
                <strong>3. Cadangan Berkala:</strong> Karena data berada di browser perangkat ini, gunakan tombol <em>Ekspor Data Pribadi</em> di bawah sebelum menghapus data browser atau berganti perangkat.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </button>

        <input
          type="file"
          accept=".json,application/json"
          className="hidden"
          id="backup-file-input"
          onChange={handleBackupFile}
        />

        <div className="mt-2 divide-y divide-[#eee8df]">
          <SettingItem
            icon={Download}
            title="Ekspor Data Pribadi"
            description="Unduh data dalam format JSON"
            onClick={exportData}
          />

          <SettingItem
            icon={Upload}
            title="Cadangkan & Pulihkan"
            description="Pilih file backup untuk memulihkan data"
            onClick={() =>
              document.getElementById('backup-file-input')?.click()
            }
          />

          <SettingItem
            icon={Trash2}
            title="Bersihkan Cache Sementara"
            description="Informasi pengelolaan penyimpanan"
            onClick={() => setActiveInfo('cache')}
          />
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ABOUT */}
      <section>
        <h3 className="mb-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#8b8177]">
          Tentang Bismillah Kelar
        </h3>

        <div className="divide-y divide-[#eee8df]">
          <SettingItem
            icon={Info}
            title="Versi Aplikasi"
            description="Versi pengembangan"
            value="v0.9.5"
            onClick={() => setActiveInfo('version')}
          />

          <SettingItem
            icon={ShieldCheck}
            title="Privasi & Keamanan"
            description="Informasi pengelolaan data"
            onClick={() => setActiveInfo('privacy')}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Kartu Filosofi & Doa */}
        <div
          onClick={() => setShowPrayerEasterEgg((current) => !current)}
          className="mt-3 rounded-2xl border border-[#252525]/20 bg-[#fff2d5] p-4"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Heart
              size={18}
              className="fill-[#EF4444] text-[#EF4444]"
            />
            <p className="text-sm font-extrabold">Filosofi &amp; Doa</p>
          </div>

          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-[#5c4c2e]">
            Dirancang sebagai ruang tenang di tengah riuhnya hariâ€”membantumu menuntaskan amanah satu demi satu, dengan hati yang lapang dan langkah yang terarah.
          </p>

          {showPrayerEasterEgg && (
            <p className="mt-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-[#5c4c2e]">
              terima kasih telah berjuang /Z
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveInfo('feedback')}
          className="mt-4 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] px-4 py-3 text-sm font-extrabold shadow-[3px_3px_0px_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
        >
          <MessageSquare size={17} />
          Beri Umpan Balik
        </button>
      </section>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <footer className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 pt-3 text-center">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-extrabold uppercase tracking-widest text-[#8b8177]">
          <Sparkles size={14} />
          Bismillah Kelar
          <Sparkles size={14} />
        </div>

        <p className="text-xs text-[#999188]">
          Ditenagai ketekunan &amp; doa {profileName}.
        </p>

        <p className="text-[10px] text-[#aaa198]">
          Tanpa beban, fokus bertumbuh.
        </p>
      </footer>

      {/* OPTION MODAL */}
      {selectedOption &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#252525]/40 px-4 py-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
            onClick={() => setActiveOption(null)}
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-labelledby="option-modal-title"
              className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-4 shadow-[5px_5px_0px_#252525]"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <h3
                    id="option-modal-title"
                    className="text-base font-extrabold"
                  >
                    {selectedOption.title}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-[#888078]">
                    {selectedOption.description}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveOption(null)}
                  aria-label="Tutup pilihan"
                  className="rounded-lg p-1.5 transition hover:bg-[#eee8df]"
                >
                  <X size={18} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {selectedOption.options.map((option) => {
                  const isSelected = draftPreset === option

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={option}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setDraftPreset(option)
                        setCustomValue('')
                        setCustomError('')
                      }}
                      className={`flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border-[1.5px] px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'border-[#252525] bg-[#BDECCF] shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525]'
                          : 'border-[#e5ddd1] bg-white hover:border-[#252525] hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold">{option}</span>

                      {isSelected && (
                        <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#252525] bg-white">
                          <Check size={12} />
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  )
                })}
              </div>

              <div className="my-3 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-[#e5ddd1]" />
                <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#999188]">
                  atau atur sendiri
                </span>
                <div className="h-px flex-1 bg-[#e5ddd1]" />
              </div>

              <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                {selectedOption.label} ({selectedOption.min}â€“{selectedOption.max})
              </label>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <textarea
                  rows={1}
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  enterKeyHint="done"
                  value={customValue}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    const digitsOnly = event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '')
                    setCustomValue(digitsOnly)
                    setDraftPreset(null)
                    setCustomError('')
                  }}
                  onKeyDown={(event) => {
                    if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                      event.preventDefault()
                      saveCustomOption()
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder="Masukkan angka"
                  className="min-w-0 flex-1 resize-none overflow-x-auto whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-[#252525] outline-none transition placeholder:font-normal placeholder:text-[#9c9489] focus:bg-[#fffdf7] focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
                />

                <span className="rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#eee8df] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold">
                  {selectedOption.unit}
                </span>
              </div>

              {customError && (
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600">
                  {customError}
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveOption(null)}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={saveCustomOption}
                  className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#BDECCF] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
                >
                  <Check size={15} />
                  Simpan
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}

      {/* INFORMATION MODAL */}
      {activeInfo &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-[#252525]/40 px-5 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
            onClick={() => setActiveInfo(null)}
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 shadow-[5px_5px_0px_#252525]"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-start justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#252525] bg-[#eee8df]">
                    {(() => {
                      const Icon = infoContent[activeInfo].icon
                      return <Icon size={20} />
                    })()}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-extrabold">
                    {infoContent[activeInfo].title}
                  </h3>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveInfo(null)}
                  aria-label="Tutup"
                  className="rounded-lg p-1 hover:bg-[#eee8df]"
                >
                  <X size={19} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#6f675f]">
                {infoContent[activeInfo].description}
              </p>

              {activeInfo === 'feedback' && (
                <div className="mt-4">
                  <div className="mb-4 rounded-xl border border-[#252525]/20 bg-[#eee8df] p-3">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#888078]">
                      Dikirim ke
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 break-all text-sm font-bold">
                      zetzayn@students.unnes.ac.id
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <label className="mb-2 block text-xs font-bold">
                    Tulis masukanmu
                  </label>

                  <textarea
                    value={feedbackText}
                    onChange={(event) => setFeedbackText(event.target.value)}
                    rows={4}
                    maxLength={1500}
                    placeholder="Ceritakan saran, ide, atau kendala yang kamu temukan..."
                    className="w-full resize-none rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-3 text-sm text-[#252525] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#9c9489] focus:bg-[#fffdf7] focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
                  />

                  <p className="mt-1 text-right text-[10px] text-[#999188]">
                    {feedbackText.length}/1500
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="mt-5 flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveInfo(null)}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold"
                >
                  Tutup
                </button>

                {activeInfo === 'feedback' && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={sendFeedback}
                    disabled={!feedbackText.trim()}
                    className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <Mail size={16} />
                    Kirim
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}

      {/* RESTORE CONFIRMATION */}
      {showRestoreConfirm &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[110] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#252525]/40 px-5 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
            onClick={() => {
              setShowRestoreConfirm(false)
              setPendingBackup(null)
            }}
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 shadow-[5px_5px_0px_#252525]"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <div className="mb-4 flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-[#252525] bg-[#BDECCF]">
                <FileJson size={22} />
              </div>

              <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold">
                Pulihkan Data?
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-[#6f675f]">
                Data saat ini akan digantikan dengan isi file backup yang
                dipilih. Pastikan kamu sudah memiliki salinan data terbaru
                sebelum melanjutkan.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-5 flex gap-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setShowRestoreConfirm(false)
                    setPendingBackup(null)
                  }}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={restoreBackup}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#BDECCF] px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold shadow-[2px_2px_0px_#252525]"
                >
                  Pulihkan
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import {
  CalendarDays,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  Clock3,
  Flame,
  BookOpen,
  Brain,
  Pencil,
  Check,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { useAppData } from '../hooks/AppContext'

type Period = 'Pekan Ini' | 'Bulan Ini'

const weekLabels = ['Sen', 'Sel', 'Rab', 'Kam', 'Jum', 'Sab', 'Min']

const habitColorCycle = [
  '#FFE69A', // 1. Krem
  '#CDE8D3', // 2. Hijau Pudar (seperti ceklis target selesai)
  '#D9CCFF', // 3. Ungu
  '#93C5FD', // 4. Biru
  '#FFB5A7', // 5. Merah Muda
  '#FB923C', // 6. Oranye
]

function getHabitColor(title: string, index: number) {
  if (index < 3) {
    const normalized = title.toLowerCase()

    if (
      normalized.includes('mengaji') ||
      normalized.includes('quran') ||
      normalized.includes('tilawah')
    ) {
      return '#FFE69A'
    }

    if (
      normalized.includes('olahraga') ||
      normalized.includes('lari') ||
      normalized.includes('workout') ||
      normalized.includes('gym')
    ) {
      return '#CDE8D3'
    }

    if (
      normalized.includes('tidur') ||
      normalized.includes('istirahat')
    ) {
      return '#D9CCFF'
    }
  }

  return habitColorCycle[index % habitColorCycle.length]
}

const emptyReflection = {
  good: '',
  improve: '',
  next: '',
}

const milestones = [
  {
    title: 'Konsistensi 14 Hari',
    description: 'Membangun rutinitas menyelesaikan target harian.',
    requirement:
      'Selesaikan minimal 1 target atau agenda pada 14 hari berbeda dalam periode berjalan.',
    unit: 'hari produktif',
    target: 14,
    icon: Flame,
    color: '#FFE69A',
  },
  {
    title: 'Pembelajar Konsisten',
    description: 'Meluangkan waktu untuk membaca dan belajar.',
    requirement:
      'Tuntaskan minimal 5 target dalam periode berjalan.',
    unit: 'target selesai',
    target: 5,
    icon: BookOpen,
    color: '#BDECCF',
  },
  {
    title: 'Fokus Mendalam',
    description: 'Menyelesaikan sesi fokus tanpa gangguan.',
    requirement:
      'Selesaikan minimal 5 sesi timer fokus Pomodoro hingga tuntas dalam periode berjalan.',
    unit: 'sesi fokus',
    target: 5,
    icon: Brain,
    color: '#D9CCFF',
  },
]

const pad = (value: number) => String(value).padStart(2, '0')

function getDateKey(date: Date) {
  return `${date.getFullYear()}-${pad(date.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getDate())}`
}

function getMonday(date: Date) {
  const result = new Date(date)
  result.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
  result.setDate(result.getDate() - ((result.getDay() + 6) % 7))
  return result
}

function formatShortDayMonth(date: Date) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
  }).format(date)
}

export default function Progress() {
  const { data, updateDailyRecord } = useAppData()
  const profileName = data.profile.name.trim() || 'teman'

  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<Period>('Pekan Ini')
  const [showPeriod, setShowPeriod] = useState(false)
  const [selectedMilestoneIndex, setSelectedMilestoneIndex] = useState<
    number | null
  >(null)

  const weekStartKey = getDateKey(getMonday(new Date()))

  const reflection =
    data.dailyRecords[weekStartKey]?.reflection ?? emptyReflection

  const [editingReflection, setEditingReflection] = useState(false)

  const [draftReflection, setDraftReflection] = useState({
    ...emptyReflection,
  })

  const now = new Date()
  const todayKey = getDateKey(now)

  const monday = getMonday(now)
  const sunday = new Date(monday)
  sunday.setDate(monday.getDate() + 6)

  const monthStart = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1)
  const monthEnd = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0)

  const periodStart = period === 'Pekan Ini' ? monday : monthStart
  const periodEnd = period === 'Pekan Ini' ? sunday : monthEnd

  const periodStartKey = getDateKey(periodStart)
  const periodEndKey = getDateKey(periodEnd)

  const allTasks = data.tasks

  const allAgenda = Object.entries(data.agenda).flatMap(
    ([date, items]) =>
      items.map((item) => ({
        ...item,
        date,
      })),
  )

  const periodTasks = allTasks.filter(
    (task) =>
      task.date >= periodStartKey &&
      task.date <= periodEndKey,
  )

  const periodAgenda = allAgenda.filter(
    (item) =>
      item.date >= periodStartKey &&
      item.date <= periodEndKey,
  )

  const completedTasks = periodTasks.filter(
    (task) => task.status === 'done',
  )

  const completedAgenda = periodAgenda.filter(
    (item) => item.done,
  )

  const totalCompleted = completedTasks.length + completedAgenda.length

  const productiveDates = new Set([
    ...completedTasks.map((task) => task.date),
    ...completedAgenda.map((item) => item.date),
  ])

  const productiveDays = productiveDates.size

  const periodRecords = Object.entries(data.dailyRecords).filter(
    ([date]) =>
      date >= periodStartKey &&
      date <= periodEndKey,
  )

  const totalFocusSessions = periodRecords.reduce(
    (sum, [, record]) => sum + (record.focusSessions ?? 0),
    0,
  )

  const totalFocusMinutes = periodRecords.reduce(
    (sum, [, record]) => sum + (record.focusMinutes ?? 0),
    0,
  )

  const averageFocusMinutes =
    totalFocusSessions > 0
      ? Math.round(totalFocusMinutes / totalFocusSessions)
      : 0

  const weekRange = `${formatShortDayMonth(monday)} â€“ ${formatShortDayMonth(sunday)}`

  const monthlyLabel = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('id-ID', {
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(now)

  const elapsedDays =
    period === 'Pekan Ini'
      ? Math.min(
          7,
          Math.max(
            0,
            Math.floor(
              (new Date(
                now.getFullYear(),
                now.getMonth(),
                now.getDate(),
              ).getTime() - monday.getTime()) / 86400000,
            ) + 1,
          ),
        )
      : now.getDate()

  const habitCount = data.habits.length

  const completedHabitPoints = Array.from(
    { length: elapsedDays },
    (_, index) => {
      const date = new Date(periodStart)
      date.setDate(periodStart.getDate() + index)

      const record = data.dailyRecords[getDateKey(date)]

      return data.habits.filter(
        (habit) => record?.habitStatus?.[habit.id] === true,
      ).length
    },
  ).reduce((sum, value) => sum + value, 0)

  const totalHabitPoints = habitCount * elapsedDays

  const habitPercentage =
    totalHabitPoints > 0
      ? Math.round(
          (completedHabitPoints / totalHabitPoints) * 100,
        )
      : 0

  const chartData = useMemo(() => {
    if (period === 'Pekan Ini') {
      return Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => {
        const date = new Date(monday)
        date.setDate(monday.getDate() + index)

        const key = getDateKey(date)

        const tasksDone = completedTasks.filter(
          (task) => task.date === key,
        ).length

        const agendaDone = completedAgenda.filter(
          (item) => item.date === key,
        ).length

        return {
          label: weekLabels[index],
          value: tasksDone + agendaDone,
        }
      })
    }

    return Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => {
      const start = new Date(
        now.getFullYear(),
        now.getMonth(),
        index * 7 + 1,
      )

      const end = new Date(
        now.getFullYear(),
        now.getMonth(),
        Math.min((index + 1) * 7, monthEnd.getDate()),
      )

      const startKey = getDateKey(start)
      const endKey = getDateKey(end)

      const tasksDone = completedTasks.filter(
        (task) =>
          task.date >= startKey &&
          task.date <= endKey,
      ).length

      const agendaDone = completedAgenda.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.date >= startKey &&
          item.date <= endKey,
      ).length

      return {
        label: `Pekan ${index + 1}`,
        value: tasksDone + agendaDone,
      }
    })
  }, [
    period,
    completedTasks,
    completedAgenda,
    monday,
    now,
    monthEnd,
  ])

  const maxChartValue = Math.max(
    1,
    ...chartData.map((item) => item.value),
  )

  const averageActivities = (
    totalCompleted /
    Math.max(1, elapsedDays)
  ).toFixed(1)

  const weekDays = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, index) => {
    const date = new Date(monday)
    date.setDate(monday.getDate() + index)

    return {
      date,
      key: getDateKey(date),
    }
  })

  function toggleHabit(habitId: number, dateKey: string) {
    const currentRecord = data.dailyRecords[dateKey]
    const currentStatus =
      currentRecord?.habitStatus?.[habitId] ?? false

    updateDailyRecord(dateKey, {
      habitStatus: {
        [habitId]: !currentStatus,
      },
    })
  }

  function saveReflection() {
    updateDailyRecord(weekStartKey, {
      reflection: { ...draftReflection },
    })

    setEditingReflection(false)
  }

  const milestoneCurrentValues = [
    productiveDays,
    completedTasks.length,
    totalFocusSessions,
  ]

  const milestoneStatus = milestones.map((item, index) =>
    milestoneCurrentValues[index] >= item.target
      ? 'Tercapai'
      : 'Dalam Proses',
  )

  const activeMilestone =
    selectedMilestoneIndex !== null
      ? {
          ...milestones[selectedMilestoneIndex],
          current: milestoneCurrentValues[selectedMilestoneIndex],
          status: milestoneStatus[selectedMilestoneIndex],
        }
      : null

  return (
    <div className="space-y-7 pb-24">
      {/* Header, Filter Pekan Ini & Ringkasan Capaian */}
      <section className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-[25px] font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight">
            Progres
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#77716a]">
            Lihat perkembangan kecil yang berarti, {profileName}.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-0.5">
          <div className="relative shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowPeriod(!showPeriod)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#FFE69A] px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition hover:bg-[#ffdf7e] active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
            >
              <CalendarDays size={14} />
              {period}
              <ChevronDown size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} />
            </button>

            {showPeriod && (
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-11 z-30 w-36 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-2 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
                {(['Pekan Ini', 'Bulan Ini'] as Period[]).map((item) => (
                  <button
                    key={item}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setPeriod(item)
                      setShowPeriod(false)
                    }}
                    className={`w-full rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs ${
                      period === item
                        ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] font-bold'
                        : 'hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {item}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <span className="inline-flex items-center justify-center whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#252525] px-9 py-2 text-xs font-extrabold text-[#FFE69A] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#D9CCFF]">
            {period === 'Pekan Ini' ? weekRange : monthlyLabel}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Ringkasan Capaian (Header di luar bingkai kiri atas konsisten dengan fitur lainnya) */}
        <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base font-bold">
              Ringkasan Capaian
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-4 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#93C5FD] p-3 shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]">

            <span>0 aktivitas</span>

          <div className="flex h-44 items-end justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#e9e1d5] pb-2">
            {chartData.map((item) => {
              const height = (item.value / maxChartValue) * 100

              return (
                <div
                  key={item.label}
                  className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-end gap-2"
                >
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-[#514b43]">
                    {item.value}
                  </span>

                  <div
                    className={`w-full max-w-7 rounded-t-md transition-all ${
                      item.value > 0
                        ? 'border border-[#252525] bg-[#93C5FD] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                        : 'bg-[#e8e3da]'

                    style={{
                      height: `${Math.max(height, 3)}%`,

                  />

                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#77716a]">
                    {item.label}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )

          </div>

          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[10px] text-[#999188]">
            Berdasarkan target dan agenda yang ditandai selesai.
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Statistik Sesi Fokus */}
      <section className="space-y-3">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-base font-bold">
            Statistik Sesi Fokus
          </h2>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
          <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#FB923C] p-4 text-[#252525] shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-white text-[#7c2d12]">
              <Clock3 size={18} />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
              {totalFocusMinutes}
              <span className="ml-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#252525]/85">
                menit
              </span>
            </p>
            <p className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#7c2d12]">
              Total waktu fokus
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] p-4 text-[#252525] shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-[#252525] bg-white text-[#14532d]">
              <Brain size={18} />
            </div>
            <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
              {totalFocusSessions}
            </p>
            <p className="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#14532d]">
              Sesi fokus selesai

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">

          </h2>

            Terhubung langsung dengan Rutinitas Harian di Today.

        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-3.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">

              {completedHabitPoints}/{totalHabitPoints} aktivitas

            <div

              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
              className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-[480px] space-y-4 overflow-y-auto rounded-t-2xl border-t-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 pb-8 shadow-2xl sm:rounded-2xl sm:border-2 sm:shadow-[5px_5px_0_#252525]"
            >
              <div className="mx-auto h-1 w-12 rounded-full bg-[#d8d0c4] sm:hidden" />

                <h2 className="text-base font-bold">
                  Refleksi Pekan Ini
                </h2>

                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setEditingReflection(false)}
                  aria-label="Tutup refleksi"
                  className="rounded-full p-2 hover:bg-[#f0ece4]"
                >
                  <X size={19} />
                </button>
              </div>

              {[
                {
                  key: 'good' as const,
                  label: 'Yang berjalan baik',
                  badgeClass: 'bg-[#4ADE80] text-[#14532d]',
                  placeholder: 'Hal-hal yang patut disyukuri pekan ini...',
                },
                {
                  key: 'improve' as const,
                  label: 'Perlu dievaluasi',
                  badgeClass: 'bg-[#FFB5A7] text-[#252525]',
                  placeholder: 'Kendala atau rutinitas yang perlu diperbaiki...',
                },
                {
                  key: 'next' as const,
                  label: 'Fokus berikutnya',
                  badgeClass: 'bg-[#FFE69A] text-[#252525]',
                  placeholder: 'Prioritas utama untuk pekan depan...',
                },
              ].map((item) => (
                <div key={item.key} className="space-y-2">
                  <label className="inline-block">

                      className={`inline-block rounded-lg border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-extrabold ${item.badgeClass}`}

                      {item.label}
                    </span>
                  </label>

                    rows={2}
                    placeholder={item.placeholder}
                    value={draftReflection[item.key]}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setDraftReflection((current) => ({
                        ...current,
                        [item.key]: event.target.value,
                      }))
                    }
                    className="w-full resize-none rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-3 text-sm text-[#252525] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#9c9489] focus:bg-[#fffdf7] focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
                  />
                </div>
              ))}

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setEditingReflection(false)}
                  className="rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-[#77716a] hover:bg-[#f0ece4]"
                >
                  Batal
                </button>

                  type="button"

                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#252525] px-5 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-[3px_3px_0_#fae195] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none sm:flex-none"
                >
                  Simpan Refleksi
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import {
  Search,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Check,
  Clock3,
  CalendarDays,
  X,
  Pencil,
  Trash2,
} from 'lucide-react'

import { useAppData } from '../hooks/AppContext'
import type { Task, TaskPriority } from '../types/app'

type FilterType = 'Semua' | 'Hari Ini' | 'Selesai' | 'Terlewat' | 'Mendatang'

function getDateString(date: Date) {
  return [
    date.getFullYear(),
    String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
    String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'),
  ].join('-')
}

function formatDate(date: string) {
  const todayString = getDateString(new Date())

  const tomorrow = new Date()
  tomorrow.setDate(tomorrow.getDate() + 1)
  const tomorrowString = getDateString(tomorrow)

  if (date === todayString) return 'Hari ini'
  if (date === tomorrowString) return 'Besok'

  return new Date(`${date}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('id-ID', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
  })
}

const filters: FilterType[] = [
  'Semua',
  'Hari Ini',
  'Selesai',
  'Terlewat',
  'Mendatang',
]

const categoryOptions = [
  'Kuliah',
  'Pribadi',
  'Rutinitas',
  'Kesehatan',
  'Kajian',
]

const priorityOptions: TaskPriority[] = ['Tinggi', 'Sedang', 'Rendah']

const priorityStyle = {
  Tinggi: 'bg-[#ffdad6] text-[#93000a]',
  Sedang: 'bg-[#fae195] text-[#554507]',
  Rendah: 'bg-[#ece8e0] text-[#55514b]',
}

const categoryStyle: Record<string, string> = {
  Kuliah: 'bg-[#e7deff] text-[#4a406b]',
  Pribadi: 'bg-[#d7cafd] text-[#4a406b]',
  Rutinitas: 'bg-[#ece8e0] text-[#55514b]',
  Kesehatan: 'bg-[#bdeccf] text-[#24543a]',
  Kajian: 'bg-[#ffe6cf] text-[#79502b]',
}

export default function Tasks() {
  const { data, updateTask, deleteTask } = useAppData()
  const tasks = data.tasks

  const todayString = getDateString(new Date())

  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState<FilterType>('Hari Ini')
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [showSort, setShowSort] = useState(false)
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<'Tanggal' | 'Prioritas'>('Tanggal')

  // State CRUD Edit & Hapus
  const [taskActionMode, setTaskActionMode] = useState<
    'normal' | 'edit' | 'delete'
  >('normal')
  const [editingTask, setEditingTask] = useState<Task | null>(null)
  const [editTitle, setEditTitle] = useState('')
  const [editCategory, setEditCategory] = useState('Pribadi')
  const [editPriority, setEditPriority] = useState<TaskPriority>('Sedang')
  const [editDate, setEditDate] = useState(todayString)
  const [editTime, setEditTime] = useState('09:00')

  const total = tasks.length

  const completed = tasks.filter(
    (task) => task.status === 'done',
  ).length

  const overdue = tasks.filter(
    (task) => task.status === 'todo' && task.date < todayString,
  ).length

  const inProgress = tasks.filter(
    (task) => task.status === 'todo' && task.date >= todayString,
  ).length

  const filteredTasks = useMemo(() => {
    let result = tasks.filter((task) =>
      task.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()),
    )

    if (activeFilter === 'Hari Ini') {
      result = result.filter(
        (task) => task.date === todayString && task.status === 'todo',
      )
    } else if (activeFilter === 'Selesai') {
      result = result.filter((task) => task.status === 'done')
    } else if (activeFilter === 'Terlewat') {
      result = result.filter(
        (task) => task.date < todayString && task.status === 'todo',
      )
    } else if (activeFilter === 'Mendatang') {
      result = result.filter(
        (task) => task.date > todayString && task.status === 'todo',
      )
    }

    if (sortBy === 'Tanggal') {
      result.sort((a, b) =>
        `${a.date} ${a.time}`.localeCompare(`${b.date} ${b.time}`),
      )
    } else {
      const priorityOrder = {
        Tinggi: 0,
        Sedang: 1,
        Rendah: 2,
      }

      result.sort(
        (a, b) => priorityOrder[a.priority] - priorityOrder[b.priority],
      )
    }

    return result
  }, [tasks, activeFilter, search, sortBy, todayString])

  const todayTasks = filteredTasks.filter(
    (task) => task.date === todayString && task.status === 'todo',
  )

  const upcomingTasks = filteredTasks.filter(
    (task) => task.date > todayString && task.status === 'todo',
  )

  const overdueTasks = filteredTasks.filter(
    (task) => task.date < todayString && task.status === 'todo',
  )

  const completedTasks = filteredTasks.filter(
    (task) => task.status === 'done',
  )

  function openEditTaskModal(task: Task) {
    setEditingTask(task)
    setEditTitle(task.title)
    setEditCategory(task.category)
    setEditPriority(task.priority)
    setEditDate(task.date)
    setEditTime(task.time)
    setTaskActionMode('normal')
  }

  function handleSaveEditedTask(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()

    if (!editingTask) return

    const trimmedTitle = editTitle.trim()
    if (!trimmedTitle) return

    updateTask(editingTask.id, {
      title: trimmedTitle,
      category: editCategory,
      priority: editPriority,
      date: editDate || todayString,
      time: editTime || '09:00',
    })

    setEditingTask(null)
  }

  function handleTaskInteraction(task: Task) {
    if (taskActionMode === 'edit') {
      openEditTaskModal(task)
      return
    }

    if (taskActionMode === 'delete') {
      const confirmed = window.confirm(
        `Hapus target "${task.title}"?`,
      )

      if (confirmed) {
        deleteTask(task.id)
      }

      setTaskActionMode('normal')
      return
    }

    updateTask(task.id, {
      status: task.status === 'done' ? 'todo' : 'done',
    })
  }

  function TaskActionButtons() {
    return (
      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() =>
            setTaskActionMode((current) =>
              current === 'edit' ? 'normal' : 'edit',
            )
          }
          aria-label="Edit target"
          aria-pressed={taskActionMode === 'edit'}
          title="Edit target"
          className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] text-[#252525] transition active:translate-x-[2px] active:translate-y-[2px] active:shadow-none ${
            taskActionMode === 'edit'
              ? 'translate-x-[2px] translate-y-[2px] bg-[#f7d874] shadow-none'
              : 'bg-[#ffe69a] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
          }`}
        >
          <Pencil size={17} />
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() =>
            setTaskActionMode((current) =>
              current === 'delete' ? 'normal' : 'delete',
            )
          }
          aria-label="Hapus target"
          aria-pressed={taskActionMode === 'delete'}
          title="Hapus target"
          className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] text-[#252525] transition active:translate-x-[2px] active:translate-y-[2px] active:shadow-none ${
            taskActionMode === 'delete'
              ? 'translate-x-[2px] translate-y-[2px] bg-[#ff9e8d] shadow-none'
              : 'bg-[#ffb5a7] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
          }`}
        >
          <Trash2 size={17} />
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  function TaskRow({ task }: { task: Task }) {
    const done = task.status === 'done'
    const isOverdue = !done && task.date < todayString

    return (
      <div
        role="button"
        tabIndex={0}
        aria-pressed={done}
        onClick={() => handleTaskInteraction(task)}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
            event.preventDefault()
            handleTaskInteraction(task)
          }
        }}
        className={`group flex cursor-pointer select-none items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] px-3 py-2.5 shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525] transition-all active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none ${
          taskActionMode === 'edit'
            ? 'bg-[#fff3c4]'
            : taskActionMode === 'delete'
              ? 'bg-[#ffe0d9]'
              : done
                ? 'bg-[#CDE8D3]'
                : isOverdue
                  ? 'bg-[#FFE6E1]'
                  : 'bg-white'
        }`}
      >
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={`flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] transition ${
            taskActionMode === 'edit'
              ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525]'
              : taskActionMode === 'delete'
                ? 'bg-[#ffb5a7] text-[#252525]'
                : done
                  ? 'bg-[#252525] text-white'
                  : 'bg-white shadow-[1.5px_1.5px_0_#252525]'
          }`}
        >
          {taskActionMode === 'edit' ? (
            <Pencil size={12} strokeWidth={2.5} />
          ) : taskActionMode === 'delete' ? (
            <Trash2 size={12} strokeWidth={2.5} />
          ) : (
            done && <Check size={14} strokeWidth={2.8} />
          )}
        </span>

        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
            <span
              className={`rounded-md border border-[#252525]/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-bold leading-none ${
                done
                  ? 'bg-white/75 text-[#4a5d4e]'
                  : (categoryStyle[task.category] ?? 'bg-[#ece8e0]')
              }`}
            >
              {task.category}
            </span>

            {!done && (
              <span
                className={`rounded-md border border-[#252525]/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-extrabold leading-none ${
                  priorityStyle[task.priority]
                }`}
              >
                {task.priority}
              </span>
            )}

            {isOverdue && (
              <span className="rounded-md border border-[#252525] bg-[#FFB5A7] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9.5px] font-extrabold leading-none text-[#252525]">
                Terlewat
              </span>
            )}

            <span
              className={`ml-auto flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                done
                  ? 'text-[#4a5d4e]'
                  : isOverdue
                    ? 'text-[#5f160c]'
                    : 'text-[#77716a]'
              }`}
            >
              <Clock3 size={11} />
              <span>
                {done ? 'Selesai' : formatDate(task.date)}, {task.time} WIB
              </span>
            </span>
          </div>

          <p
            className={`mt-1 text-sm font-bold leading-snug ${
              done ? 'text-[#252525]/75 line-through' : 'text-[#252525]'
            }`}
          >
            {task.title}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  function TodaySection({ showEmptyState }: { showEmptyState: boolean }) {
    if (!showEmptyState && todayTasks.length === 0) return null

    return (
      <section className="mb-5">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="pt-0.5">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[#7c2d12]">
                Target Hari Ini
              </h2>
              <span className="rounded-full border border-[#252525]/20 bg-[#FB923C] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
                {todayTasks.length}
              </span>
            </div>

            <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-medium text-[#77716a]">
              Output dari Tambah Target
            </span>
          </div>

          <TaskActionButtons />
        </div>

        {todayTasks.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#c7beb0] bg-white px-4 py-10 text-center">
            <CalendarDays size={28} className="mx-auto text-[#aaa197]" />

            <h3 className="mt-2.5 text-sm font-bold">
              Belum ada target hari ini
            </h3>

            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#77716a]">
              Tekan tombol + Tambah Target di bawah untuk menambahkan target baru.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {todayTasks.map((task) => (
              <TaskRow key={task.id} task={task} />
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative space-y-4 pb-24 pr-[3px]">
      {/* 1. Judul Target + Keterangan */}
      <section>
        <h1 className="text-[25px] font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight">
          Target
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-[#77716a]">
          Manajemen tugas dan produktivitas mandiri
        </p>
      </section>

      {/* 2. Search satu baris bersebelahan dengan Urutkan */}
      <section className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search
            size={16}
            className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#77716a]"
          />

          <textarea
            rows={1}
            enterKeyHint="search"
            value={search}
            onChange={(event) =>
              setSearch(event.target.value.replace(/\r?\n/g, ''))
            }
            onKeyDown={(event) => {
              if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                event.preventDefault()
                event.currentTarget.blur()
              }
            }}
            placeholder="Cari target pribadi..."
            className="block w-full resize-none overflow-x-auto whitespace-nowrap rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white py-2.5 pl-9 pr-8 text-sm text-[#252525] outline-none transition placeholder:text-[#9c9489] focus:bg-[#fffdf7] focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
          />

          {search && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearch('')}
              aria-label="Hapus pencarian"
              className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full p-1 text-[#77716a] hover:bg-[#f0ece4] hover:text-[#252525]"
            >
              <X size={14} />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="relative shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setShowSort(!showSort)}
            aria-label="Urutkan target"
            aria-expanded={showSort}
            className="flex h-[44px] items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 text-xs font-extrabold shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
          >
            <SlidersHorizontal size={15} />
            <span>{sortBy}</span>
          </button>

          {showSort && (
            <div className="absolute right-0 top-12 z-20 w-44 rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white p-2 shadow-[4px_4px_0_#252525]">
              <p className="px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-[#77716a]">
                URUTKAN BERDASARKAN
              </p>

              {(['Tanggal', 'Prioritas'] as const).map((item) => (
                <button
                  key={item}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setSortBy(item)
                    setShowSort(false)
                  }}
                  className={`w-full rounded-lg px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-xs ${
                    sortBy === item
                      ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] font-bold'
                      : 'hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                  }`}
                >
                  {item}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 3. Indikator Status Full Kiri-Kanan: Total -> Berjalan -> Selesai -> Terlewat */}
      <section className="grid w-full grid-cols-4 gap-2">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-white px-1.5 py-2 shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]">
          <span className="text-sm font-extrabold leading-tight text-[#252525]">
            {total}
          </span>
          <span className="mt-1 rounded-full bg-[#f1ede6] px-2 py-0.5 text-[8px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#252525]">
            Total
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col items-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#FB923C] px-1.5 py-2 shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]">
          <span className="text-sm font-extrabold leading-tight text-[#252525]">
            {inProgress}
          </span>
          <span className="mt-1 rounded-full bg-white/90 px-2 py-0.5 text-[8px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#7c2d12]">
            Berjalan
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col items-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#4ADE80] px-1.5 py-2 shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]">
          <span className="text-sm font-extrabold leading-tight text-[#252525]">
            {completed}
          </span>
          <span className="mt-1 rounded-full bg-white/90 px-2 py-0.5 text-[8px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#14532d]">
            Selesai
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col items-center rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] bg-[#FFB5A7] px-1.5 py-2 shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]">
          <span className="text-sm font-extrabold leading-tight text-[#252525]">
            {overdue}
          </span>
          <span className="mt-1 rounded-full bg-white/90 px-2 py-0.5 text-[8px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wide text-[#5f160c]">
            Terlewat
          </span>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* 4. Filter Satu Baris Horizontal (Ukuran Tombol Tetap, Hanya Font Diperkecil) */}
      <section
        data-no-swipe
        className="mx-auto flex w-fit max-w-full flex-nowrap items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto px-0.5 py-0.5 [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden"
      >
        {filters.map((filter) => {
          const count =
            filter === 'Semua'
              ? total
              : filter === 'Hari Ini'
                ? tasks.filter(
                    (task) =>
                      task.date === todayString && task.status === 'todo',
                  ).length
                : filter === 'Selesai'
                  ? completed
                  : filter === 'Terlewat'
                    ? overdue
                    : tasks.filter(
                        (task) =>
                          task.date > todayString && task.status === 'todo',
                      ).length

          const active = activeFilter === filter

          return (
            <button
              key={filter}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setActiveFilter(filter)
                setTaskActionMode('normal')
              }}
              style={{
                fontSize: '9.5px',
                lineHeight: 1,
                padding: '6px 10px',
              }}
              className={`inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap rounded-full border border-[#252525] font-bold transition-all duration-150 ${
                active
                  ? 'translate-x-[1px] translate-y-[1px] border-[#252525]/75 bg-[#f7e5ab] text-[#252525]/80 shadow-none'
                  : 'bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525] shadow-[1px_1px_0_#252525] hover:bg-[#ffdf7e] active:translate-x-[1px] active:translate-y-[1px] active:bg-[#f7e5ab] active:shadow-none'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{filter}</span>
              <span
                style={{
                  fontSize: '8px',
                  lineHeight: 1,
                  minWidth: '16px',
                  height: '16px',
                  padding: '0 4px',
                }}
                className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-[#252525]/25 font-extrabold ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-[#252525] text-[#FFE69A]'
                    : 'bg-white text-[#252525]'
                }`}
              >
                {count}
              </span>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </section>

      {/* Banner Mode Edit / Hapus Target */}
      {taskActionMode !== 'normal' && (
        <p className="rounded-lg bg-[#f5f0e7] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-[#77716a]">
          {taskActionMode === 'edit'
            ? 'Pilih target yang ingin diedit.'
            : 'Pilih target yang ingin dihapus.'}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTaskActionMode('normal')}
            className="ml-2 font-bold text-[#252525] underline"
          >
            Batal
          </button>
        </p>
      )}

      {/* 5. Daftar Output Target sesuai Filter */}
      <div className="pt-1">
        {/* Urutan Filter Semua: Target Hari Ini -> Terlewat -> Mendatang & Studi -> Telah Diselesaikan */}
        {activeFilter === 'Semua' && <TodaySection showEmptyState={false} />}

        {/* Filter Hari Ini */}
        {activeFilter === 'Hari Ini' && <TodaySection showEmptyState={true} />}

        {/* Bagian Terlewat */}
        {overdueTasks.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mb-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[#5f160c]">
                  Terlewat
                </h2>

                <span className="rounded-full border border-[#252525]/20 bg-[#FFB5A7] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
                  {overdueTasks.length}
                </span>
              </div>

              <TaskActionButtons />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              {overdueTasks.map((task) => (
                <TaskRow key={task.id} task={task} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Bagian Mendatang & Studi */}
        {upcomingTasks.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mb-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[#7c2d12]">
                  Mendatang &amp; Studi
                </h2>

                <span className="rounded-full border border-[#252525]/20 bg-[#FB923C] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-extrabold text-[#252525]">
                  {upcomingTasks.length}
                </span>
              </div>

              <TaskActionButtons />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              {upcomingTasks.map((task) => (
                <TaskRow key={task.id} task={task} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Bagian Telah Diselesaikan */}
        {completedTasks.length > 0 && (
          <section className="mb-5">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-[#1e4620]">
                  Telah Diselesaikan
                </h2>

                <span className="rounded-full border border-[#252525]/20 bg-[#CDE8D3] px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-[#1e4620]">
                  {completedTasks.length}
                </span>
              </div>

              <TaskActionButtons />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              {completedTasks.map((task) => (
                <TaskRow key={task.id} task={task} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {/* Tampilan Kosong Khusus Filter Selesai / Terlewat / Mendatang */}
        {activeFilter === 'Selesai' && completedTasks.length === 0 && (
          <section>
            <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#c7beb0] bg-white px-4 py-10 text-center">
              <CalendarDays size={28} className="mx-auto text-[#aaa197]" />

              <h3 className="mt-2.5 text-sm font-bold">
                Belum ada target selesai
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#77716a]">
                Target yang sudah kamu centang selesai akan muncul di sini.
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {activeFilter === 'Terlewat' && overdueTasks.length === 0 && (
          <section>
            <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#c7beb0] bg-white px-4 py-10 text-center">
              <CalendarDays size={28} className="mx-auto text-[#aaa197]" />

              <h3 className="mt-2.5 text-sm font-bold">
                Tidak ada target terlewat
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#77716a]">
                Semua target sebelumnya sudah tertangani dengan baik.
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {activeFilter === 'Mendatang' && upcomingTasks.length === 0 && (
          <section>
            <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-[#c7beb0] bg-white px-4 py-10 text-center">
              <CalendarDays size={28} className="mx-auto text-[#aaa197]" />

              <h3 className="mt-2.5 text-sm font-bold">
                Belum ada target mendatang
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[#77716a]">
                Tekan tombol + Tambah Target di bawah untuk menjadwalkan target berikutnya.
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Modal Edit Target */}
      {editingTask &&
        createPortal(
          <div
            onClick={() => setEditingTask(null)}
            className="fixed inset-0 z-[120] flex items-center justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-[#252525]/40 px-5 py-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
          >
            <div
              role="dialog"
              aria-modal="true"
              aria-labelledby="edit-task-modal-title"
              onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
              className="my-auto max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-sm overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#fffaf1] p-5 shadow-[5px_5px_0_#252525]"
            >
              <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3
                  id="edit-task-modal-title"
                  className="text-base font-bold"
                >
                  Edit Target
                </h3>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setEditingTask(null)}
                  aria-label="Tutup"
                  className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-[#f0ece4] hover:bg-[#e5dfd3]"
                >
                  <X size={17} />
                </button>
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={handleSaveEditedTask} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                    Nama target
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    autoFocus
                    maxLength={80}
                    value={editTitle}
                    onChange={(event) => setEditTitle(event.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Tulis nama target..."
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-[#252525] outline-none focus:shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525]"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                    Kategori
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {categoryOptions.map((cat) => (
                      <button
                        key={cat}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setEditCategory(cat)}
                        className={`rounded-lg border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold transition ${
                          editCategory === cat
                            ? 'bg-[#D9CCFF] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                            : 'bg-white hover:bg-[#f5f0e7]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {cat}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                    Prioritas
                  </label>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    {priorityOptions.map((prio) => (
                      <button
                        key={prio}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setEditPriority(prio)}
                        className={`rounded-xl border-[1.5px] border-[#252525] py-2 text-xs font-extrabold transition ${
                          editPriority === prio
                            ? prio === 'Tinggi'
                              ? 'bg-[#ffb5a7] text-[#5f160c] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                              : prio === 'Sedang'
                                ? 'bg-[#ffe69a] text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                                : 'bg-[#ece8e0] text-[#252525] shadow-[2px_2px_0_#252525]'
                            : 'bg-white text-[#77716a]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {prio}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                  <div>
                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                      Tanggal
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="date"
                      value={editDate}
                      onChange={(event) => setEditDate(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#252525] outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-bold">
                      Waktu (WIB)
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="time"
                      value={editTime}
                      onChange={(event) => setEditTime(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-white px-2.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#252525] outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="flex w-full items-center justify-center rounded-xl border-2 border-[#252525] bg-[#ffe69a] px-4 py-3 text-sm font-extrabold text-[#252525] shadow-[3px_3px_0_#252525] transition active:translate-x-0.5 active:translate-y-0.5 active:shadow-none"
                >
                  Simpan Perubahan
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </div>,
          document.body,
        )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'

  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Circle,

function getDateString(date: Date) {
  return [
    date.getFullYear(),
    String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
    String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0'),
  ].join('-')
}

function formatTime(seconds: number) {
  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60)
  const remainingSeconds = seconds % 60

  return `${String(minutes).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(
    remainingSeconds,
  ).padStart(2, '0')}`
}

const habitIconMap: Record<string, LucideIcon> = {
